            Load_Popup_Settings();
            // This line of code is generated by Data Source Configuration Wizard
            // Fill a SqlDataSource
            sqlDataSource1.Fill();
        }

        private void create_new_btn_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            UC_Opener_Form form = new UC_Opener_Form("New Aggrement", new New_Aggrment());
            form.Show(this);
        }

        public void Load_Popup_Settings()
        {
            int width = this.Width;
            int height = 280;
            customer_search_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            stores_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            items_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
        }

        private void refresh_btn_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            aggrement_id_inp.Text = "";
            by_customer_check.CheckState = CheckState.Unchecked;
            by_date_check.CheckState = CheckState.Unchecked;
            stores_check.CheckState = CheckState.Unchecked;
            item_chkbx.CheckState = CheckState.Unchecked;

            sqlDataSource1.Queries.Remove(sqlDataSource1.Queries["Query"]);
            CustomSqlQuery query = new CustomSqlQuery();
            query.Sql = @"select TOP 5000 stores.area_code as 'Area Code',aggrements.date  as 'Date',aggrements.id as 'Code',aggrements.ref_no as 'Ref.No',accounts.id as 'Cust.Code',accounts.name as 'Customer',guardians.name as 'Guarantor',aggrements.current_balance as 'Balance',items.id as 'Item.Code',items.name as 'Item',sale_items.sale_rate as 'Rate',sold_qty as 'Qty',sale_items.net_total as 'Total' from ((((dbo.aggrements aggrements left join dbo.accounts accounts on (accounts.id = aggrements.customer_id))  inner join dbo.stores stores on (stores.id = aggrements.store_id)) left join dbo.accounts guardians on (guardians.id = aggrements.guardian_id))) left join sale_items on aggrements.id=sale_items.agreement_id left join items on sale_items.item_id=items.id ORDER BY aggrements.id DESC";
            query.Name = "Query";
            sqlDataSource1.Queries.Add(query);
            sqlDataSource1.RebuildResultSchema();
            sqlDataSource1.Fill();

            Set_Sum();
        }

        private void gridView1_DoubleClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (gridView1.DataRowCount > 0)
            {

                GridHitInfo hitInfo = gridView1.CalcHitInfo((e as MouseEventArgs).Location);
                if (!(hitInfo.InGroupRow))
                {
                    int aggrement_id = Convert.ToInt32(gridView1.GetFocusedRowCellValue(gridView1.Columns["Code"]));
                    Edit_Aggrement edit_aggrement = new Edit_Aggrement(aggrement_id);
                    UC_Opener_Form form = new UC_Opener_Form("Agreement Details", edit_aggrement);
                    edit_aggrement.uc_opener = form;
                    form.Show(this);
                }
            }
        }

        private void by_customer_check_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (by_customer_check.Checked)
            {
                customer_search_cmb.Enabled = true;
                aggrement_id_inp.Text = null;
                aggrement_id_inp.Enabled = false;
            }
            else
            {
                customer_search_cmb.Text = null;
                customer_search_cmb.Enabled = false;
                if(by_date_check.Checked != true && stores_check.Checked != true)
                {
                    aggrement_id_inp.Enabled = true;
                    by_date_check.Enabled = true;
                }
            }
        }

        private void by_date_check_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (by_date_check.Checked)
            {
                from_date_picker.Enabled = true;
                to_date_picker.Enabled = true;
                aggrement_id_inp.Text = null;
                aggrement_id_inp.Enabled = false;
                from_date_picker.Focus();
            }
            else
            {
                from_date_picker.Enabled = false;
                to_date_picker.Enabled = false;
                from_date_picker.Text = null;
                to_date_picker.Text = null;
                if(by_customer_check.Checked != true)
                {
                    aggrement_id_inp.Enabled = true;
                }
            }
        }
        public void Search_By_Aggrement_ID()
        {
            sqlDataSource1.Queries.Remove(sqlDataSource1.Queries["Query"]);
            CustomSqlQuery query = new CustomSqlQuery();
            query.Sql = @"select aggrements.date  as 'Date',aggrements.id as 'Code',aggrements.ref_no as 'Ref.No',stores.area_code as 'Area Code',accounts.id as 'Customer Code',accounts.name as 'Customer Name',guardians.name as 'Guardian Name',aggrements.current_balance as 'Balance',aggrements.status as 'Status' from ((((dbo.aggrements aggrements left join dbo.accounts accounts on (accounts.id = aggrements.customer_id))  inner join dbo.stores stores on (stores.id = aggrements.store_id)) left join dbo.accounts guardians on (guardians.id = aggrements.guardian_id))) WHERE aggrements.ref_no='" + aggrement_id_inp.Text+ "' ORDER BY aggrements.id DESC";
            query.Name = "Query";
            sqlDataSource1.Queries.Add(query);
            sqlDataSource1.RebuildResultSchema();
            sqlDataSource1.Fill();
        }

            query.Name = "Query";
            sqlDataSource1.Queries.Add(query);

        private void search_btn_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if(stores_check.Checked == true && stores_cmb.Text == "" && by_customer_check.CheckState!= CheckState.Checked && item_chkbx.CheckState!=CheckState.Checked)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select store !");
                stores_cmb.ShowPopup();
                stores_cmb.Focus();
            }else if(stores_check.Checked == true && stores_cmb.Text != "" && by_customer_check.CheckState != CheckState.Checked && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                //search by store
                Search_By_Store_ID();
            }
            else if (stores_check.Checked == true && stores_cmb.Text != "" && by_date_check.Checked == true && (from_date_picker.Text =="" || to_date_picker.Text == "") && by_customer_check.CheckState != CheckState.Checked && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select from date and to date carefully !");
            }
            else if (stores_check.Checked == true && stores_cmb.Text != "" && by_date_check.Checked == true && (from_date_picker.Text != "" || to_date_picker.Text != "") && by_customer_check.CheckState != CheckState.Checked && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                //search by store and dates
                Search_By_Store_And_Date();
            }
            else if(aggrement_id_inp.Enabled == true && aggrement_id_inp.Text == "" && by_customer_check.CheckState != CheckState.Checked && stores_check.CheckState != CheckState.Checked && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter aggrement id !");
                aggrement_id_inp.Focus();
            }else if(aggrement_id_inp.Enabled == true && aggrement_id_inp.Text != "" && by_customer_check.CheckState != CheckState.Checked && stores_check.CheckState != CheckState.Checked && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                Search_By_Aggrement_ID();
            }else if(by_customer_check.Checked == true && by_date_check.Checked == false && customer_search_cmb.Text == "" && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select customer !");
                customer_search_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }else if(by_customer_check.Checked == true && by_date_check.Checked == false && customer_search_cmb.Text != "" && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                Search_By_Customer();
            }
            else if(by_customer_check.Checked == true && by_date_check.Checked == true && customer_search_cmb.Text == "" && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select customer !");
                customer_search_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }else if (by_customer_check.Checked == true && by_date_check.Checked == true && customer_search_cmb.Text != "" && (from_date_picker.Text == "" || to_date_picker.Text == "") && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select from date and to date properly !");
            }
            else if(by_customer_check.Checked == true && by_date_check.Checked == true && customer_search_cmb.Text != "" && from_date_picker.Text != "" && to_date_picker.Text != "" && item_chkbx.CheckState != CheckState.Checked)
            {
                Search_By_Customer_And_Date();
            }
            //here item wise start
            //[item,customer],[item,customer,date]
            else if (by_customer_check.Checked == false && by_date_check.Checked == false && item_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked && stores_check.Checked==false)
            {
                if(items_cmb.Text == "")
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select item properly !");
                }
                else
                {
                    Search_By_Item_ID();
                }
            }
            else if (by_customer_check.Checked == false && by_date_check.Checked == true && item_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked)
            {
                if (items_cmb.Text == "")
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select item properly !");
                }
                else
                {
                    if(from_date_picker.Text =="" || to_date_picker.Text == "")
                    {
                        XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select from date and to date properly !");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Search_By_Item_And_Date();
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (by_customer_check.Checked == false && by_date_check.Checked == false && item_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked && stores_check.Checked == false)
            {
                if (items_cmb.Text == "")
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select item properly !");
                }
                else
                {
                    if (from_date_picker.Text == "" || to_date_picker.Text == "")
                    {
                        XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select from date and to date properly !");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Search_By_Item_And_Date();
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (by_customer_check.Checked == false && by_date_check.Checked == false && item_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked && stores_check.Checked == true)
            {
                if (items_cmb.Text == "")
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select item properly !");
                }
                else
                {
                    if (stores_cmb.Text == "")
                    {
                        XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select store properly !");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Search_By_Store_AND_Item();
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (by_customer_check.Checked == false && by_date_check.CheckState == CheckState.Checked && item_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked && stores_check.CheckState == CheckState.Checked)
            {
                if (items_cmb.Text == "")
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select item properly !");
                }
                else
                {
                    if (stores_cmb.Text == "")
                    {
                        XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select store properly !");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if(from_date_picker.Text =="" || to_date_picker.Text == "")
                        {
                            XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select from date and to date properly !");
                        }
                        else
                        {

                            Search_By_Store_AND_Item_Date();
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (by_customer_check.Checked == true && by_date_check.Checked == false && item_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked && stores_check.Checked == false)
            {
                if (items_cmb.Text == "")
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select item properly !");
                }
                else
                {
                    if (customer_search_cmb.Text == "")
                    {
                        XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select customer properly !");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Search_By_Customer_AND_Item();
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (by_customer_check.Checked == true && by_date_check.Checked == true && item_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked && stores_check.Checked == false)
            {
                if (items_cmb.Text == "")
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select item properly !");
                }
                else
                {
                    if (customer_search_cmb.Text == "")
                    {
                        XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select customer properly !");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if(from_date_picker.Text =="" || to_date_picker.Text == "")
                        {
                            XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select date properly !");
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            Search_By_Customer_AND_Item_Date();
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            //here item wise close
            else
            {
                Search_By_Date();
            }
            Set_Sum();
        }
        public void Set_Sum()
        {
            DevExpress.XtraGrid.GridSummaryItem debit_item = gridView1.Columns["Total"].SummaryItem;
            debit_item.SummaryType = DevExpress.Data.SummaryItemType.Sum;
            Double debit_total = Convert.ToDouble(debit_item.SummaryValue);
            total_balance_lbl.Text = string.Format("{0:#,##0.00}", Double.Parse(debit_total.ToString()));

            DevExpress.XtraGrid.GridSummaryItem items_qty = gridView1.Columns["Qty"].SummaryItem;
            items_qty.SummaryType = DevExpress.Data.SummaryItemType.Sum;
            int total_items = Convert.ToInt32(items_qty.SummaryValue);
            items_count_lbl.Text = total_items.ToString();
        }
        private void stores_check_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (stores_check.Checked)
            {
                stores_cmb.Enabled = true;
                aggrement_id_inp.Text = null;
                aggrement_id_inp.Enabled = false;
            }
            else
            {
                stores_cmb.Text = null;
                stores_cmb.Enabled = false;
                aggrement_id_inp.Text = null;
                aggrement_id_inp.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private void gridView1_RowCountChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            Set_Sum();
        }

        private void item_chkbx_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (item_chkbx.Checked)
            {
                items_cmb.Enabled = true;
                aggrement_id_inp.Text = null;
                aggrement_id_inp.Enabled = false;
            }
            else
            {
                items_cmb.Text = null;
                items_cmb.Enabled = false;
                aggrement_id_inp.Text = null;
                aggrement_id_inp.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private void gridView1_GroupLevelStyle(object sender, DevExpress.XtraGrid.Views.Grid.GroupLevelStyleEventArgs e)
        {
            switch (e.Level)
            {
                case 0:
                    e.LevelAppearance.Options.UseBackColor = true;
                    e.LevelAppearance.BackColor2 = Color.FromArgb(0, 153, 204);
                    e.LevelAppearance.ForeColor = Color.White;
                    e.LevelAppearance.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(0, 34, 204);
                    break;
                case 1:
                    e.LevelAppearance.Options.UseBackColor = true;
                    e.LevelAppearance.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(221, 0, 221);
                    e.LevelAppearance.ForeColor = Color.White;
                    e.LevelAppearance.BackColor2 = Color.FromArgb(255, 153, 102);
                    break;
                case 2:
                    e.LevelAppearance.Options.UseBackColor = true;
                    e.LevelAppearance.ForeColor = Color.White;
                    e.LevelAppearance.BackColor = Color.FromArgb(0,102, 102);
                    e.LevelAppearance.BackColor2 = Color.FromArgb(0, 255,255);
                    break;
                    //default:
                    //    //do nothing  
                    //break;

            }
        }

        public void Load_Popup_Settings()
        {
            int width = this.Width;
            int height = 280;
            store_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            customer_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            guarrantor_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            model_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            item_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);

        }

        public void Send_Message_To_Numbers(int tmp_store_id, String Group_Name, String Message_Type, string Message_Text, String SMS_Gateway, String Phone_Option, String Home_Phone, String Office_Phone, int Customer_Code, String Customer_Name, String Ref_No, Double Agreement_Total, Double Advance_Amount, Double Customer_Balance, int No_Of_Installments, string Items_List)
        {
            bool is_home_phone_selected = false;
            bool is_office_phone_selected = false;
            if (Phone_Option == "Both")
            {
                is_home_phone_selected = true;
                is_office_phone_selected = true;
            }
            else if (Phone_Option == "Office Phone")
            {
                is_office_phone_selected = true;
            }
            else if (Phone_Option == "Home Phone")
            {
                is_home_phone_selected = true;
            }
           
            Console.WriteLine("Initial Message Text:" + Message_Text);
            string text_message = Message_Text;
            string text_message_name = text_message.Replace("[NAME]", Customer_Name);
            string text_message_ref_no = text_message_name.Replace("[REF_NO]", Ref_No);
            string text_message_items_list = text_message_ref_no.Replace("[ITEMS_LIST]", Items_List);
            string text_message_agreement_total = text_message_items_list.Replace("[AGREEMENT_TOTAL]", Convert.ToInt32(Agreement_Total).ToString());
            string text_message_advance_amount = text_message_agreement_total.Replace("[ADVANCE_AMOUNT]", Convert.ToInt32(Advance_Amount).ToString());
            string text_message_customer_balance = text_message_advance_amount.Replace("[CUSTOMER_BALANCE]", Convert.ToInt32(Customer_Balance).ToString());
            string final_processed_text = text_message_customer_balance.Replace("[NO_OF_INSTALLMENTS]", No_Of_Installments.ToString());
            Console.WriteLine("Message Textafter Processing:" + final_processed_text);
            if (is_office_phone_selected == true && Office_Phone != null && Office_Phone != "")
            {
                Messages.Message message = new Messages.Message();
                message.ACCOUNT_ID = Customer_Code;
                message.Store_Id = tmp_store_id;
                message.Message_Text = final_processed_text;
                message.Message_Type = Message_Type;
                message.Group = Group_Name;
                message.SMS_Gateway = SMS_Gateway;
                message.RECIEVER_NUMBER =Office_Phone;
                goto send_message_start;
            send_message_start:
                int is_sent = -1;
                is_sent = messages_controller.Send_Message(message);
                if (is_sent == 1)
                {
                    message.Status = "Sent";
                    Console.WriteLine("Message sent susscessfuly !");
                }
                else if (is_sent == -3)
                {
                    Thread.Sleep(1000);
                    goto send_message_start;
                }
                else
                {
                    message.Status = "Draft";
                    Console.WriteLine("Message Drafted !");
                }
                messages_controller.Store_Message(message);
            }
            if (is_home_phone_selected == true && Home_Phone != null && Home_Phone != "")
            {
                Messages.Message message = new Messages.Message();
                message.ACCOUNT_ID = Customer_Code;
                message.Store_Id = tmp_store_id;
                message.Message_Text = final_processed_text;
                message.Message_Type = Message_Type;
                message.Group = Group_Name;
                message.SMS_Gateway = SMS_Gateway;
                message.RECIEVER_NUMBER = Home_Phone;
                goto send_message_start;
            send_message_start:
                int is_sent = -1;
                is_sent = messages_controller.Send_Message(message);
                if (is_sent == 1)
                {
                    message.Status = "Sent";
                    Console.WriteLine("Message sent susscessfuly !");
                }
                else if (is_sent == -3)
                {
                    Thread.Sleep(2000);
                    goto send_message_start;
                }
                else
                {
                    message.Status = "Draft";
                    Console.WriteLine("Message Drafted !");
                }
                messages_controller.Store_Message(message);
            }
        Console.WriteLine("Message sending task is completed !");
        SMS_Thread.Abort();
        }

        public void Calculate_EMI_Dues()
        {
            if (balance_inp.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter above fields !");

            } else if (first_emi_inp.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter first emi amount !");
                first_emi_inp.Focus();
            }
            else if (start_datepicker.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select start date !");
                start_datepicker.Focus();
            }
            else if (frequency_inp.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter frequency input !");
                frequency_inp.Focus();
            }
            else
            {
                int frequency = 0;
                String start_date;
                Double balance = 0;
                Double repeat_balance = 0;
                Double first_emi = 0;
                Double rest_emi = 0;
                emi_amounts_table.Clear();
                rem_emi_amounts_table.Clear();
                // Previously Paid Dues
                emi_amounts_table = aggrements_controller.Get_Aggrement_Paid_Dues_Table(TargetAgreementId);

                // Sale Return Adjustment Row
                DataRow ndr = rem_emi_amounts_table.NewRow();
                ndr["EMI No"] = ret_emi_no_inp.Text;
                ndr["EMI Amount"] = repeat_balance;
                ndr["Due Date"] = return_date_picker.Text;
                ndr["Paid Date"] = return_date_picker.Text;
                ndr["Balance"] = Convert.ToDouble(balance_inp.Text);
                ndr["Remarks"] = "Sale Return Adjustment";
                rem_emi_amounts_table.Rows.Add(ndr);

                repeat_balance = 0;

                // Remaining Dues
                start_date = start_datepicker.Text;
                frequency = Convert.ToInt32(frequency_inp.Text);
                balance = Convert.ToDouble(balance_inp.Text);
                repeat_balance = balance;
                rest_emi = first_emi = Convert.ToDouble(first_emi_inp.Text);
                int rows_count = emi_amounts_table.Rows.Count; 
                int i = 0;

                while (repeat_balance > 0)
                {
                    DataRow dr = rem_emi_amounts_table.NewRow();
                    dr["EMI No"] = rows_count + 1;
                    if (i == 0)
                    {
                        dr["Due Date"] = start_date;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        DateTime emi_date = Convert.ToDateTime(start_date).AddMonths(i);
                        dr["Due Date"] = emi_date.ToString("dd-MM-yyyy");
                    }

                    if (repeat_balance > first_emi)
                    {
                        dr["EMI Amount"] = first_emi;
                        repeat_balance = repeat_balance - first_emi;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        dr["EMI Amount"] = repeat_balance;
                        repeat_balance = 0;
                    }

                    rem_emi_amounts_table.Rows.Add(dr);

                    i++;
                    rows_count++;
                }

                foreach (DataRow row in rem_emi_amounts_table.Rows)
                {
                    emi_amounts_table.ImportRow(row);
                }

                emi_dues_list.DataSource = emi_amounts_table;
            }
        }

        public void Save()
        {
            if (TargetAgreementId == -1 || gridView7.RowCount == 0) return;

            double moneyKept = 0;
            double.TryParse(advance_payment_inp.Text, out moneyKept);

            double newEmi = 0;
            double.TryParse(first_emi_inp.Text, out newEmi);

            bool success = aggrements_controller.Store_Agreement_Return_Final(
                TargetAgreementId,
                ref_no_inp.Text,
                DateTime.Now,
                agreement_items_table,
                moneyKept,
                newEmi,
                rem_emi_amounts_table,
                Convert.ToDouble(balance_inp.Text),
                Convert.ToDouble(remaining_bal_inp.Text),
                Convert.ToDouble(total_amount_inp.Text)
            );

            if (success)
            {
                Reset_Fields();
            }
        }

        public void Save_Old()
        {
            if (return_date_picker.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select date !");
                return_date_picker.ShowPopup();
            } else if (customer_cmb.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select or add customer !");
                customer_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }
            else if (store_cmb.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select or add store !");
                store_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }
            else if (agreement_items_table.Rows.Count <= 0)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select or add item !");
                item_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }
            else if (emi_amounts_table.Rows.Count <= 0)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter EMI plan !");
            }
            else
            {
                int emi_no = -1;
                String ref_no = "";
                if (store_cmb.Text != "")
                {
                    store_id = Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue);
                }

                if (ref_no_inp.Text != "")
                {
                    ref_no = ref_no_inp.Text;
                }
                Aggrements_Controller aggrements_controller = new Aggrements_Controller();
                int guardian_id = -1;
                
                int aggrement_id = aggrements_controller.Store(Convert.ToDateTime(return_date_picker.Text), Convert.ToInt32(customer_cmb.EditValue), store_id, guardian_id, Convert.ToDouble(total_amount_inp.Text), Convert.ToDouble(advance_payment_inp.Text), Convert.ToDouble(balance_inp.Text), agreement_items_table, emi_amounts_table.Rows.Count, Convert.ToDouble(first_emi_inp.Text), Convert.ToDouble(first_emi_inp.Text), start_datepicker.Text, emi_no, ref_no, remarks_inp.Text);
                if (aggrement_id > 0)
                {
                    bool res = aggrements_controller.Store_Dues(aggrement_id, emi_amounts_table);
                    if (res == true)
                    {
                        if (send_msg_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked)
                        {
                            if (SMS_Thread.ThreadState == ThreadState.Unstarted || SMS_Thread.ThreadState == ThreadState.Aborted || SMS_Thread.ThreadState==ThreadState.Stopped)
                            {
                                // settings values for message
                                string items_list_str_builder = null;
                                for (int i = 0; i < gridView7.RowCount; i++)
                                {
                                    items_list_str_builder = items_list_str_builder + gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, "Quantity");
                                    items_list_str_builder = items_list_str_builder + " " + gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, "Item");
                                    if(i < (gridView7.RowCount - 1))
                                    {
                                        items_list_str_builder = items_list_str_builder + ", ";
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        items_list_str_builder = items_list_str_builder + " ";
                                    }
                                }
                                Accounts.Account customer= accounts_controller.Get_Customer(Convert.ToInt32(customer_cmb.EditValue));
                                int temp_store_id = -1;
                                String Group_Name = null;
                                String Message_Type = null;
                                String Message_Text = null;
                                String SMS_Gateway = null;
                                String Phone_Options = "Both";
                                if (store_cmb.Text != "")
                                {
                                    store_id = Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue);
                                }
                                if(store_cmb.Text != "")
                                {
                                    temp_store_id = Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue);
                                }
                                Group_Name = "Customers ( Debitors )";

                                if (sms_gateway_cmb.Text != "")
                                {
                                    SMS_Gateway = sms_gateway_cmb.Text;
                                }
                                if (phones_options_cmb.Text != "")
                                {
                                    Phone_Options = phones_options_cmb.Text;
                                }
                                int customer_code = customer.Account_Id;
                                String Customer_Name = customer.Account_Name;
                                String Home_Phone =customer.Home_Phone;
                                String Office_Phone = customer.Office_Phone;
                                Message_Text = message_inp.Text;
                                Message_Type = messages_type_cmb.Text;
                                Message_Text = message_inp.Text;
                                String Ref_No = ref_no_inp.Text;
                                Double Total_amt =Convert.ToDouble(total_amount_inp.Text);
                                int No_of_installments = emi_dues_container.RowCount;
                                Double Customer_Balance=Convert.ToDouble(balance_inp.Text);
                                Message_Type = messages_type_cmb.Text;
                                SMS_Gateway = sms_gateway_cmb.Text;
                                Double Paid_Amt =Convert.ToDouble(advance_payment_inp.Text);
                                //-------------------------------------
                                SMS_Thread = new Thread(() => Send_Message_To_Numbers(temp_store_id, Group_Name, Message_Type, Message_Text, SMS_Gateway,Phone_Options,Home_Phone,Office_Phone,customer.Account_Id,customer.Account_Name,Ref_No,Total_amt,Paid_Amt,Customer_Balance,No_of_installments,items_list_str_builder));
                                SMS_Thread.Start();
                            }
                        }
                        Reset_Fields();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        public void setEvaluatedValues()
        {
            Double single_changed_temp_total = 0;
            if (item_qty_inp.Text != "" && sale_rate_inp.Text != "")
            {
                var qty = Convert.ToDouble(item_qty_inp.Text);
                var sale_rate_val = Convert.ToDouble(sale_rate_inp.Text);
                var item_total_paid = Convert.ToDouble(item_total_paid_inp.Text);
                Double discount_value = 0;
                single_changed_temp_total = (qty * sale_rate_val);
                item_total.Text = single_changed_temp_total.ToString();
                item_rem_inp.Text = (single_changed_temp_total - item_total_paid).ToString();
            }
        }
        private void item_cmb_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            
            if (store_cmb.Text != "" && ref_no_inp.Text != "")
            {
                GridView view = item_cmb.Properties.View;
                string sale_rate_fieldName = "Sale Rate"; // or other field name  
                Double org_sale_rate = Convert.ToDouble(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue), sale_rate_fieldName));
                string item_id_fieldName = "Code"; // or other field name  
                int item_id = Convert.ToInt32(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue),item_id_fieldName));
                string qty_fieldName = "Sold Qty"; // or other field name  
                int qty = Convert.ToInt32(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue), qty_fieldName));
                model_cmb.EditValue = store_item.Model_Id;
                sale_rate_inp.Text = org_sale_rate.ToString();
                item_qty_inp.Text = qty.ToString();
                Max_Item_Qty = qty;
                item_total.Text = Convert.ToInt32((org_sale_rate * qty)).ToString();
                item_qty_inp.Focus();
            }
            else
            {
                Max_Item_Qty = 0;
                stock_in_lbl.Text = "0.00";
                value_lbl.Text = "0.00";
                item_cmb.Text = null;
                item_qty_inp.Text = null;
                sale_rate_inp.Text = null;
                item_total.Text = null;
                panelControl7.Visible = false;
            }
            
        }

        private void sale_rate_inp_KeyPress(object sender, KeyPressEventArgs e)
        {
            Char ch = e.KeyChar;

            if (!(Char.IsDigit(ch) || ch == 13 || ch == 8 || ch == 46))
            {
                e.Handled = true;
            }

            int dot_counter = sale_rate_inp.Text.Count(f => f == '.');

            if ((sale_rate_inp.Text.Length <= 0 && ch == 46) || (dot_counter > 0 && ch == 46))
            {
                e.Handled = true;
            }

        }

            }

        public bool If_Item_Exists_In_Cart(int sr_no)
        {
            if (gridView7.DataRowCount > 0)
            {
                bool check_exists = false;

                
                for (int i = 0; i < gridView7.DataRowCount; i++)
                {
                    if (Convert.ToInt32(gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, gridView7.Columns["Sr.No"])) == sr_no)
                    {
                        check_exists = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                return check_exists;
            }
            else
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
        public void Reset_Item_Entries()
        {
            item_cmb.Clear();
            item_qty_inp.Text = null;
            sale_rate_inp.Text = "";
            model_cmb.Text = null;
            stock_in_lbl.Text = "0.00";
            value_lbl.Text = "0.00";
            item_total.Text = "";
            item_total_paid_inp.Text = "";
            item_rem_inp.Text = "";
            
        }
        
        public void Refresh_Summary_Calculations()
        {
            // 1. Sum up Items in the return grid
            double totalReturnItemValue = 0;
            double totalPaidAmount = 0;
            double totalRemainingBalance = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < gridView7.RowCount; i++)
            {
                totalReturnItemValue += Convert.ToDouble(gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, "Total"));
                totalPaidAmount += Convert.ToDouble(gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, "Paid"));
                totalRemainingBalance += Convert.ToDouble(gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, "Remaining"));
            }
            total_amount_inp.Text = totalReturnItemValue.ToString("0.00");
            advance_payment_inp.Text = totalPaidAmount.ToString("0.00");
            total_rem_inp.Text = totalRemainingBalance.ToString("0.00");

            // 2. Get Header Info
            double currentDebt = 0;
            double.TryParse(remaining_bal_inp.Text, out currentDebt);

            double moneyAlreadyPaidForReturningItem = 0;
            double.TryParse(advance_payment_inp.Text, out moneyAlreadyPaidForReturningItem);

            // 3. New Balance Calculation
            // We give customer credit for the UNPAID part of the item: (Total - Paid)
            double creditAmount = totalReturnItemValue - moneyAlreadyPaidForReturningItem;
            double proposedNewBalance = currentDebt - creditAmount;

            // Set bottom balance for EMI generation
            balance_inp.Text = proposedNewBalance.ToString("0.00");
        }
        public void Add_Item_To_Cart()
        {
            if(item_cmb.Text != "")
            {
                if (If_Item_Exists_In_Cart(Convert.ToInt32(item_cmb.EditValue)) == true)
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Item already exists in cart !");
                    Reset_Item_Entries();
                    item_cmb.ShowPopup();
                }
                else
                {
                    if (Convert.ToDouble(item_qty_inp.Text) <= Max_Item_Qty)
                    {
                        DataRow dr = agreement_items_table.NewRow();
                        GridView view = item_cmb.Properties.View;
                        string fieldName = "Code"; // or other field name  
                        int item_code = Convert.ToInt32(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue), fieldName));
                        dr["Code"] = item_code;
                        dr["Item"] = item_cmb.Text;
                        dr["Model"] = model_cmb.Text;
                        dr["Quantity"] = Convert.ToDouble(item_qty_inp.Text);
                        dr["Sale Rate"] = Convert.ToDouble(sale_rate_inp.Text);
                        dr["Total"] = Convert.ToDouble(item_total.Text);
                        dr["Paid"] = (item_total_paid_inp.Text == "") ? 0 : Convert.ToDouble(item_total_paid_inp.Text);
                        dr["Remaining"] = (item_rem_inp.Text == "") ? 0 : Convert.ToDouble(item_rem_inp.Text);
                        dr["Original Sale Rate"] = this.orignal_sale_rate;
                        agreement_items_table.Rows.Add(dr);
                        Reset_Item_Entries();
                        this.orignal_sale_rate = 0;
                        Refresh_Summary_Calculations();
                        ret_emi_no_inp.Focus();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        XtraMessageBox.Show("You can return maximum "+Max_Item_Qty+" quantity of this item !");
                        item_qty_inp.Focus();
                    }

                }
            }
        }

        private void item_qty_inp_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Control && e.KeyCode == Keys.D)

        public void Refresh_Final_Values_Old()
        {
            GridSummaryItem summery_item = gridView7.Columns["Total"].SummaryItem;
            summery_item.SummaryType = DevExpress.Data.SummaryItemType.Sum;
            Double sub_totals = Convert.ToDouble(summery_item.SummaryValue);
            total_amount_inp.Text = string.Format("{0:#,##0.00}", Double.Parse(sub_totals.ToString()));
        }
        private void gridView7_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if ((e.KeyCode == Keys.Delete) || (e.Control && e.KeyCode == Keys.X))
            {
                gridView7.DeleteRow(gridView7.FocusedRowHandle);
                if (gridView7.DataRowCount > 0)
                {
                    Refresh_Summary_Calculations();
                }
                else
                {
                    Reset_Fields();
                }
            }
            else if ((e.Control && e.KeyCode == Keys.E) || (e.KeyCode == Keys.F2) && items_list_container.ContainsFocus == true)
            {
                if (gridView7.DataRowCount > 0)
                {
                    Int32 sr_no = Convert.ToInt32(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Sr.No"));
                    //XtraMessageBox.Show(Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Code")));
                    item_cmb.EditValue = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Code"));
                    sale_rate_inp.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Sale Rate"));
                    //model_cmb.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Model"));
                    item_qty_inp.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Quantity"));
                    item_total.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Total"));
                    item_total_paid_inp.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Paid"));
                    item_rem_inp.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Remaining"));
                    GridView view = item_cmb.Properties.View;
                    string fieldName = "Code";
                    int item_code = Convert.ToInt32(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue), fieldName));
                    Items.Item item = items_controller.Get_Item(item_code);
                    model_cmb.EditValue = item.Model_Id;

                    int row_ind = gridView7.GetFocusedDataSourceRowIndex();
                    gridView7.DeleteRow(row_ind);
                    Refresh_Summary_Calculations();
                    item_qty_inp.Focus();
                }
            }
            else
            {
                //Controls_List(sender, e);
            }
        }

        private void New_Aggrment_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            panelControl5.Size = new Size(panelControl5.Width, panelControl5.Height - panelControl6.Height);
            if (Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway == "API")
            {
                sms_gateway_cmb.Text = Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway;
                sms_gateway_cmb.Enabled = false;

            }
            else if (Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway == "Android Wifi")
            {
                sms_gateway_cmb.Text = Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway;
                sms_gateway_cmb.Enabled = false;
            }
            else if (Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway == "None")
            {
                sms_gateway_cmb.Text = Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway;
                sms_gateway_cmb.Enabled = false;
                send_msg_chkbx.Enabled = false;

            }
            messages_type_cmb.Text = Settings.Default.default_sms_language;
            if (Settings.Default.default_sms_language != "English")
            {
                foreach (InputLanguage lng in InputLanguage.InstalledInputLanguages)
                {
                    if (lng.Culture.DisplayName == "Urdu (Islamic Republic of Pakistan)")
                    {
                        InputLanguage.CurrentInputLanguage = lng;
                    }
                }
                message_inp.RightToLeft = RightToLeft.Yes;
                message_inp.Text = Settings.Default.new_agreement_urd_template;
            }
            else
            {
                foreach (InputLanguage lng in InputLanguage.InstalledInputLanguages)
                {
                    if (lng.Culture.DisplayName == "English (United States)")
                    {
                        InputLanguage.CurrentInputLanguage = lng;
                    }
                }
                message_inp.RightToLeft = RightToLeft.No;
                message_inp.Text = Settings.Default.new_agreement_eng_template;
            }
        }

        public void Load_Sale_Data(string Ref_No)
        {
            Aggrement agg = aggrements_controller.Get_Aggrement_By_Ref_No(Ref_No);
            if (agg.Aggrement_Id > 0)
            {
                this.TargetAgreementId = agg.Aggrement_Id; // Store this globally

                // FIX FOR CMB SELECTION: Ensure data source is filled and types match
                customer_cmb.EditValue = agg.Customer_Id;

                // Use a safe conversion to match the ValueMember of the lookup
                store_cmb.EditValue = agg.Store_Id;
                
               
                sale_date_picker.DateTime = Convert.ToDateTime(agg.Date);
                remaining_bal_inp.Text = agg.Current_Balance.ToString();

                // Total Receipts = (Total Price - Current Balance)
                total_reciepts_inp.Text = (agg.Total_Amount - (agg.Current_Balance + agg.Sale_Return_Amount)).ToString();

                // Load only items that haven't been fully returned yet
                sqlDataSource1.Queries.Remove(sqlDataSource1.Queries["store_items"]);
                CustomSqlQuery query = new CustomSqlQuery();
                query.Sql = $@"SELECT itm.id as 'Code', itm.name as 'Item', si.sale_rate as 'Sale Rate', 
                      (si.sold_qty - si.returned_quantity) as 'Sold Qty' 
                      FROM sale_items si 
                      JOIN items itm ON si.item_id = itm.id 
                      WHERE si.agreement_id = {agg.Aggrement_Id} AND (si.sold_qty - si.returned_quantity) > 0";
                query.Name = "store_items";
                sqlDataSource1.Queries.Add(query);
                sqlDataSource1.RebuildResultSchema();
                sqlDataSource1.Fill();
                emi_amounts_table.Clear();
                rem_emi_amounts_table.Clear();
                emi_amounts_table = agg.Aggrement_Dues_Table;
                emi_dues_list.DataSource = emi_amounts_table;


                item_cmb.Enabled = true;
                item_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }
            else
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Agreement not found with this reference number.");
            }
        }


        // Ensure the balance is auto-calculated but stays editable
        private void item_qty_inp_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (item_qty_inp.ContainsFocus)
            {
                double qty = 0;
                double.TryParse(item_qty_inp.Text, out qty);
                double rate = Convert.ToDouble(sale_rate_inp.Text);
                item_total.Text = (qty * rate).ToString();

                // Update the proposed new balance
                double currentAggBalance = Convert.ToDouble(remaining_bal_inp.Text);
                balance_inp.Text = (currentAggBalance - (qty * rate)).ToString();
            }
        }

        private void item_total_paid_inp_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (item_total_paid_inp.ContainsFocus == true && item_total_paid_inp.Text != "")
            {
                 setEvaluatedValues();
               
             
            }
        }

        public void Load_Popup_Settings()
        {
            int width = this.Width;
            int height = 280;
            store_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            customer_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            model_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            guardian_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);
            item_cmb.Properties.PopupFormSize = new Size(width, height);

        }

        public void Send_Message_To_Numbers(int tmp_store_id, String Group_Name, String Message_Type, string Message_Text, String SMS_Gateway, String Phone_Option, String Home_Phone, String Office_Phone, int Customer_Code, String Customer_Name, String Ref_No, Double Agreement_Total, Double Advance_Amount, Double Customer_Balance, int No_Of_Installments, string Items_List)
        {
            bool is_home_phone_selected = false;
            bool is_office_phone_selected = false;
            if (Phone_Option == "Both")
            {
                is_home_phone_selected = true;
                is_office_phone_selected = true;
            }
            else if (Phone_Option == "Office Phone")
            {
                is_office_phone_selected = true;
            }
            else if (Phone_Option == "Home Phone")
            {
                is_home_phone_selected = true;
            }
           
            Console.WriteLine("Initial Message Text:" + Message_Text);
            string text_message = Message_Text;
            string text_message_name = text_message.Replace("[NAME]", Customer_Name);
            string text_message_ref_no = text_message_name.Replace("[REF_NO]", Ref_No);
            string text_message_items_list = text_message_ref_no.Replace("[ITEMS_LIST]", Items_List);
            string text_message_agreement_total = text_message_items_list.Replace("[AGREEMENT_TOTAL]", Convert.ToInt32(Agreement_Total).ToString());
            string text_message_advance_amount = text_message_agreement_total.Replace("[ADVANCE_AMOUNT]", Convert.ToInt32(Advance_Amount).ToString());
            string text_message_customer_balance = text_message_advance_amount.Replace("[CUSTOMER_BALANCE]", Convert.ToInt32(Customer_Balance).ToString());
            string final_processed_text = text_message_customer_balance.Replace("[NO_OF_INSTALLMENTS]", No_Of_Installments.ToString());
            Console.WriteLine("Message Textafter Processing:" + final_processed_text);
            if (is_office_phone_selected == true && Office_Phone != null && Office_Phone != "")
            {
                Messages.Message message = new Messages.Message();
                message.ACCOUNT_ID = Customer_Code;
                message.Store_Id = tmp_store_id;
                message.Message_Text = final_processed_text;
                message.Message_Type = Message_Type;
                message.Group = Group_Name;
                message.SMS_Gateway = SMS_Gateway;
                message.RECIEVER_NUMBER =Office_Phone;
                goto send_message_start;
            send_message_start:
                int is_sent = -1;
                is_sent = messages_controller.Send_Message(message);
                if (is_sent == 1)
                {
                    message.Status = "Sent";
                    Console.WriteLine("Message sent susscessfuly !");
                }
                else if (is_sent == -3)
                {
                    Thread.Sleep(2000);
                    goto send_message_start;
                }
                else
                {
                    message.Status = "Draft";
                    Console.WriteLine("Message Drafted !");
                }
                messages_controller.Store_Message(message);
            }
            if (is_home_phone_selected == true && Home_Phone != null && Home_Phone != "")
            {
                Messages.Message message = new Messages.Message();
                message.ACCOUNT_ID = Customer_Code;
                message.Store_Id = tmp_store_id;
                message.Message_Text = final_processed_text;
                message.Message_Type = Message_Type;
                message.Group = Group_Name;
                message.SMS_Gateway = SMS_Gateway;
                message.RECIEVER_NUMBER = Home_Phone;
                goto send_message_start;
            send_message_start:
                int is_sent = -1;
                is_sent = messages_controller.Send_Message(message);
                if (is_sent == 1)
                {
                    message.Status = "Sent";
                    Console.WriteLine("Message sent susscessfuly !");
                }
                else if (is_sent == -3)
                {
                    Thread.Sleep(2000);
                    goto send_message_start;
                }
                else
                {
                    message.Status = "Draft";
                    Console.WriteLine("Message Drafted !");
                }
                messages_controller.Store_Message(message);
            }
        Console.WriteLine("Message sending task is completed !");
        SMS_Thread.Abort();
        }

        public void Calculate_EMI_Dues()
        {
            if (balance_inp.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter above fields !");

            } else if (first_emi_inp.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter first emi amount !");
                first_emi_inp.Focus();
            }
            else if (start_datepicker.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select start date !");
                start_datepicker.Focus();
            }
            else if (frequency_inp.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter frequency input !");
                frequency_inp.Focus();
            }
            else
            {
                int frequency = 0;
                String start_date;
                Double balance = 0;
                Double repeat_balance = 0;
                Double first_emi = 0;
                Double rest_emi = 0;
                emi_amounts_table.Clear();
                start_date = start_datepicker.Text;
                frequency = Convert.ToInt32(frequency_inp.Text);
                balance = Convert.ToDouble(balance_inp.Text);
                repeat_balance = balance;
                rest_emi = first_emi = Convert.ToDouble(first_emi_inp.Text);
                int i = 0;
                while (repeat_balance > 0)
                {
                    DataRow dr = emi_amounts_table.NewRow();
                    dr["EMI No"] = i + 1;
                    if (i == 0)
                    {
                        dr["Due Date"] = start_date;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        DateTime emi_date = Convert.ToDateTime(start_date).AddMonths(i);
                        dr["Due Date"] = emi_date.ToString("dd-MM-yyyy");
                    }

                    if (repeat_balance > first_emi)
                    {
                        dr["EMI Amount"] = first_emi;
                        repeat_balance = repeat_balance - first_emi;
                        dr["Balance"] = repeat_balance;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        dr["EMI Amount"] = repeat_balance;
                        repeat_balance = 0;
                        dr["Balance"] = repeat_balance;
                    }

                    emi_amounts_table.Rows.Add(dr);

                    i++;
                }
            }
        }

        public void Reset_Fields()
        {
            customer_cmb.Text = null;
            //store_cmb.Text = null;
            voucher_id_inp.Text = null;
            item_cmb.Text = null;
            model_cmb.Text = null;
            sale_rate_inp.Text = null;
            ref_no_inp.Text = string.Join("-", ref_no_inp.Text.Split('-').Take(2)) + "-";
            balance_inp.Text = null;
            total_amount_inp.Text = null;
            remarks_inp.Text = null;
            total_amount_inp.Text = null;
            advance_payment_inp.Text = null;
            first_emi_inp.Text = null;
            frequency_inp.Text = null;
            guardian_cmb.Text = null;
            start_datepicker.Text = null;
            stock_in_lbl.Text = "0.00";
            value_lbl.Text = "0.00";

            agreement_items_table.Clear();

            customer_cmb.Focus();
        }

        public void Save()
        {
            if (date_picker.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select date !");
                date_picker.ShowPopup();
            } else if (customer_cmb.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select or add customer !");
                customer_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }
            else if (store_cmb.Text == "")
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select or add store !");
                store_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }
            else if (agreement_items_table.Rows.Count <= 0)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please select or add item !");
                item_cmb.ShowPopup();
            }
            else if (emi_amounts_table.Rows.Count <= 0)
            {
                XtraMessageBox.Show("Please enter EMI plan !");
            }
            else
            {
                int emi_no = -1;
                String ref_no = "";
                if (store_cmb.Text != "")
                {
                    store_id = Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue);
                }

                if (voucher_id_inp.Text != "")
                {
                    emi_no = Convert.ToInt32(voucher_id_inp.Text);
                }
                if (ref_no_inp.Text != "")
                {
                    ref_no = ref_no_inp.Text;
                }
                Aggrements_Controller aggrements_controller = new Aggrements_Controller();
                int guardian_id = -1;
                if (guardian_cmb.Text != "")
                {
                    guardian_id = Convert.ToInt32(guardian_cmb.EditValue.ToString());
                }
                int aggrement_id = aggrements_controller.Store(Convert.ToDateTime(date_picker.Text), Convert.ToInt32(customer_cmb.EditValue), store_id, guardian_id, Convert.ToDouble(total_amount_inp.Text), Convert.ToDouble(advance_payment_inp.Text), Convert.ToDouble(balance_inp.Text), agreement_items_table, emi_amounts_table.Rows.Count, Convert.ToDouble(first_emi_inp.Text), Convert.ToDouble(first_emi_inp.Text), start_datepicker.Text, emi_no, ref_no, remarks_inp.Text);
                if (aggrement_id > 0)
                {
                    bool res = aggrements_controller.Store_Dues(aggrement_id, emi_amounts_table, ref_no_inp.Text);
                    if (res == true)
                    {
                        if (send_msg_chkbx.CheckState == CheckState.Checked)
                        {
                            if (SMS_Thread.ThreadState == ThreadState.Unstarted || SMS_Thread.ThreadState == ThreadState.Aborted || SMS_Thread.ThreadState==ThreadState.Stopped)
                            {
                                // settings values for message
                                string items_list_str_builder = null;
                                for (int i = 0; i < gridView7.RowCount; i++)
                                {
                                    items_list_str_builder = items_list_str_builder + gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, "Quantity");
                                    items_list_str_builder = items_list_str_builder + " " + gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, "Item");
                                    if(i < (gridView7.RowCount - 1))
                                    {
                                        items_list_str_builder = items_list_str_builder + ", ";
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        items_list_str_builder = items_list_str_builder + " ";
                                    }
                                }
                                Accounts.Account customer= accounts_controller.Get_Customer(Convert.ToInt32(customer_cmb.EditValue));
                                int temp_store_id = -1;
                                String Group_Name = null;
                                String Message_Type = null;
                                String Message_Text = null;
                                String SMS_Gateway = null;
                                String Phone_Options = "Both";
                                if (store_cmb.Text != "")
                                {
                                    store_id = Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue);
                                }
                                if(store_cmb.Text != "")
                                {
                                    temp_store_id = Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue);
                                }
                                Group_Name = "Customers ( Debitors )";

                                if (sms_gateway_cmb.Text != "")
                                {
                                    SMS_Gateway = sms_gateway_cmb.Text;
                                }
                                if (phones_options_cmb.Text != "")
                                {
                                    Phone_Options = phones_options_cmb.Text;
                                }
                                int customer_code = customer.Account_Id;
                                String Customer_Name = customer.Account_Name;
                                String Home_Phone =customer.Home_Phone;
                                String Office_Phone = customer.Office_Phone;
                                Message_Text = message_inp.Text;
                                Message_Type = messages_type_cmb.Text;
                                Message_Text = message_inp.Text;
                                String Ref_No = ref_no_inp.Text;
                                Double Total_amt =Convert.ToDouble(total_amount_inp.Text);
                                int No_of_installments = emi_dues_container.RowCount;
                                Double Customer_Balance=Convert.ToDouble(balance_inp.Text);
                                Message_Type = messages_type_cmb.Text;
                                SMS_Gateway = sms_gateway_cmb.Text;
                                Double Paid_Amt =Convert.ToDouble(advance_payment_inp.Text);
                                //-------------------------------------
                                SMS_Thread = new Thread(() => Send_Message_To_Numbers(temp_store_id, Group_Name, Message_Type, Message_Text, SMS_Gateway,Phone_Options,Home_Phone,Office_Phone,customer.Account_Id,customer.Account_Name,Ref_No,Total_amt,Paid_Amt,Customer_Balance,No_of_installments,items_list_str_builder));
                                SMS_Thread.Start();
                            }
                        }
                        Reset_Fields();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private void advance_payment_inp_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if(advance_payment_inp.ContainsFocus == true)
            {
                if(advance_payment_inp.Text != "" && total_amount_inp.Text != "")
                {
                    Double total_amount = Convert.ToDouble(total_amount_inp.Text);
                    Double advance_payment = Convert.ToDouble(advance_payment_inp.Text);
                    balance_inp.Text = (total_amount - advance_payment).ToString();
                }
            }
        }
        public void setEvaluatedValues()
        {
            Double single_changed_temp_total = 0;
            if (item_qty_inp.Text != "" && sale_rate_inp.Text != "" && disc_rs_inp.Text != "" && disc_percent_inp.Text != "")
            {
                var qty = Convert.ToDouble(item_qty_inp.Text);
                var sale_rate_val = Convert.ToDouble(sale_rate_inp.Text);
                Double discount_value = Convert.ToDouble(disc_rs_inp.Text);
                single_changed_temp_total = (qty * sale_rate_val);
                if (discount_value >= 0)
                {
                    if (discount_value > 0)
                    {
                        disc_percent_inp.Text = Math.Round(((discount_value / single_changed_temp_total) * 100), 2).ToString();
                    }
                    single_changed_temp_total = single_changed_temp_total - discount_value;
                }

                item_total.Text = single_changed_temp_total.ToString();
            }
        }
        private void item_cmb_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            
            if (item_cmb.Text != "" && store_cmb.Text != "")
            {
                Items.Stores.Stores_Controller stores_controller = new Items.Stores.Stores_Controller();
                GridView view = item_cmb.Properties.View;
                string sale_rate_fieldName = "Sale Rate"; // or other field name  
                Double org_sale_rate = Convert.ToDouble(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue), sale_rate_fieldName));
                string item_id_fieldName = "Code"; // or other field name  
                int item_id = Convert.ToInt32(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue),item_id_fieldName));
                this.store_item = stores_controller.Get_Current_Stock_Item_By_Sale_Rate(item_id,Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue), org_sale_rate);
                Items.Stores.Available_Stock_Item_Value available_item_value = stores_controller.Get_Available_Item_Value(item_id, Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue),org_sale_rate);
                stock_in_lbl.Text = available_item_value.Available_Stock.ToString();
                value_lbl.Text = available_item_value.Stock_Value.ToString();
                this.orignal_sale_rate = org_sale_rate;
                model_cmb.EditValue = store_item.Model_Id;
                item_total.Text=sale_rate_inp.Text = org_sale_rate.ToString();
                item_qty_inp.Text = "1";
                disc_percent_inp.Text = "0";
                disc_rs_inp.Text = "0";
                panelControl7.Visible = true;
                item_qty_inp.Focus();
            }
            else
            {
                stock_in_lbl.Text = "0.00";
                value_lbl.Text = "0.00";
                item_cmb.Text = null;
                item_qty_inp.Text = null;
                sale_rate_inp.Text = null;
                disc_percent_inp.Text = null;
                disc_rs_inp.Text = null;
                item_total.Text = null;
                panelControl7.Visible = false;
            }
            
        }

        private void store_cmb_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if(store_cmb.ContainsFocus == true)
            {
                if(store_cmb.Text != "")
                {
                    sqlDataSource1.Queries.Remove(sqlDataSource1.Queries["store_items"]);
                    CustomSqlQuery query = new CustomSqlQuery();
                    query.Sql = @"SELECT rank() over(order by sti.item_id,items.name,sti.sale_rate) as 'Sr.No',sti.item_id as 'Code',items.name as 'Item',models.name as 'Model',sti.sale_rate as 'Sale Rate',SUM((sti.stock_in + sti.sales_return) - (sti.sold_out + sti.returned + sti.transfered)) as 'Stock',i_u.name as 'U.O.M' FROM stock_transaction_items as sti left join items on sti.item_id=items.id left join item_units as i_u on items.unit_id=i_u.id left join item_models as models on items.model_id=models.id WHERE ((sti.stock_in - (sti.sold_out + sti.returned + sti.transfered) ) > 0) and sti.store_id='" + Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue)+"' GROUP BY sti.item_id,items.name,sti.sale_rate,i_u.name,models.name";
                    query.Name = "store_items";
                    sqlDataSource1.Queries.Add(query);
                    sqlDataSource1.RebuildResultSchema();
                    sqlDataSource1.Fill();
                    item_cmb.Refresh();
                    item_cmb.Enabled = true;
                }
                else
                {
                    item_cmb.Text = null;
                    item_cmb.Enabled = false;
                    item_qty_inp.Text = null;
                    sale_rate_inp.Text = null;
                    disc_percent_inp.Text = null;
                    disc_rs_inp.Text = null;
                    item_total.Text = null;
                }
            }
        }

        private void sale_rate_inp_KeyPress(object sender, KeyPressEventArgs e)
        {
            Char ch = e.KeyChar;

            if (!(Char.IsDigit(ch) || ch == 13 || ch == 8 || ch == 46))
            {
                e.Handled = true;
            }

            int dot_counter = sale_rate_inp.Text.Count(f => f == '.');

            if ((sale_rate_inp.Text.Length <= 0 && ch == 46) || (dot_counter > 0 && ch == 46))
            {
                e.Handled = true;
            }

            if (ch == 13)
            {
                disc_rs_inp.Focus();
            }
        }

        private void total_amount_inp_KeyPress(object sender, KeyPressEventArgs e)
        {
            Char ch = e.KeyChar;

            if (!(Char.IsDigit(ch) || ch == 13 || ch == 8 || ch == 46))
            {
                e.Handled = true;
            }

            int dot_counter = total_amount_inp.Text.Count(f => f == '.');

            if ((total_amount_inp.Text.Length <= 0 && ch == 46) || (dot_counter > 0 && ch == 46))
            {
                e.Handled = true;
            }

            if (e.KeyChar == 13)
            {
                advance_payment_inp.Focus();
            }
        }

            {
                e.Handled = true;
            }
        }

        private void item_qty_inp_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (item_qty_inp.ContainsFocus == true)
            {
                if (item_qty_inp.Text != "")
                {
                    Double item_qty = Convert.ToDouble(item_qty_inp.Text);
                    if (item_qty > 0)
                    {
                        setEvaluatedValues();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private void sale_rate_inp_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (sale_rate_inp.ContainsFocus == true)
            {
                if (sale_rate_inp.Text != "")
                {
                    Double item_qty = Convert.ToDouble(sale_rate_inp.Text);
                    if (item_qty > 0)
                    {
                        setEvaluatedValues();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private void disc_rs_inp_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (disc_rs_inp.ContainsFocus == true)
            {
                if (disc_rs_inp.Text != "" && disc_rs_inp.Text != ".")
                {
                    if (disc_rs_inp.Text == "0")
                    {
                        disc_percent_inp.Text = "0";
                    }
                    Double dir = Convert.ToDouble(disc_rs_inp.Text);
                    if (dir >= 0)
                    {
                        setEvaluatedValues();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private void disc_percent_inp_EditValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (disc_percent_inp.ContainsFocus == true)
            {
                if (disc_percent_inp.Text != "" && disc_percent_inp.Text != ".")
                {
                    Double dip = Convert.ToDouble(disc_percent_inp.Text);
                    if (dip >= 0)
                    {
                        var qty = Convert.ToDouble(item_qty_inp.Text);
                        var sale_rate_val = Convert.ToDouble(sale_rate_inp.Text);
                        Double disc_val = (qty * sale_rate_val);
                        if (dip > 0)
                        {
                            disc_rs_inp.Text = (Math.Round((disc_val * (dip / 100)), 2)).ToString();
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            disc_rs_inp.Text = "0";
                        }
                        setEvaluatedValues();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

            Char ch = e.KeyChar;

        public bool If_Item_Exists_In_Cart(int sr_no)
        {
            if (gridView7.DataRowCount > 0)
            {
                bool check_exists = false;

                
                for (int i = 0; i < gridView7.DataRowCount; i++)
                {
                    if (Convert.ToInt32(gridView7.GetRowCellValue(i, gridView7.Columns["Sr.No"])) == sr_no)
                    {
                        check_exists = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                return check_exists;
            }
            else
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
        public void Reset_Item_Entries()
        {
            item_cmb.Text = "";
            item_qty_inp.Text = null;
            sale_rate_inp.Text = "";
            model_cmb.Text = null;
            disc_rs_inp.Text = "";
            stock_in_lbl.Text = "0.00";
            value_lbl.Text = "0.00";
            disc_percent_inp.Text = "";
            item_total.Text = "";
            advance_payment_inp.Focus();
        }
        public void Set_Final_Values()
        {

            GridSummaryItem summery_item = gridView7.Columns["Total"].SummaryItem;
            summery_item.SummaryType = DevExpress.Data.SummaryItemType.Sum;
            Double sub_totals = Convert.ToDouble(summery_item.SummaryValue);
            total_amount_inp.Text = string.Format("{0:#,##0.00}", Double.Parse(sub_totals.ToString()));
            Double paid_amount_val = 0;
            Double value_after_disc = 0;
            
            if (advance_payment_inp.Text != "")
            {
                paid_amount_val = Convert.ToDouble(advance_payment_inp.Text);
            }
            balance_inp.Text = (sub_totals - paid_amount_val).ToString();
            total_amount_inp.Text = string.Format("{0:#,##0.00}", Double.Parse(value_after_disc.ToString()));
        }
        public void Add_Item_To_Cart()
        {
            if(item_cmb.Text != "")
            {
                if (If_Item_Exists_In_Cart(Convert.ToInt32(item_cmb.EditValue)) == true)
                {
                    XtraMessageBox.Show("Item already exists in cart !");
                    Reset_Item_Entries();
                    item_cmb.ShowPopup();
                }
                else
                {
                    if (Convert.ToDouble(item_qty_inp.Text) <= Convert.ToDouble(item_cmb.Properties.View.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Stock")))
                    {
                        DataRow dr = agreement_items_table.NewRow();
                        dr["Sr.No"] = Convert.ToInt32(item_cmb.EditValue);
                        GridView view = item_cmb.Properties.View;
                        string fieldName = "Code"; // or other field name  
                        int item_code = Convert.ToInt32(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue), fieldName));
                        dr["Code"] = item_code;
                        dr["Item"] = item_cmb.Text;
                        dr["Model"] = model_cmb.Text;
                        dr["Quantity"] = Convert.ToDouble(item_qty_inp.Text);
                        dr["Sale Rate"] = Convert.ToDouble(sale_rate_inp.Text);
                        dr["Disc(%)"] = Convert.ToDouble(disc_percent_inp.Text);
                        dr["Disc(R.S)"] = Convert.ToDouble(disc_rs_inp.Text);
                        dr["Total"] = Convert.ToDouble(item_total.Text);
                        dr["Original Sale Rate"] = this.orignal_sale_rate;
                        agreement_items_table.Rows.Add(dr);
                        Reset_Item_Entries();
                        this.orignal_sale_rate = 0;
                        Set_Final_Values();
                        Refresh_Final_Values();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        XtraMessageBox.Show("Quantity is not enough available !");
                        item_qty_inp.Focus();
                    }

                }
            }
        }

        private void item_qty_inp_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if (e.Control && e.KeyCode == Keys.D)
            {
                this.Add_Item_To_Cart();
            }
        }

        private void sale_rate_inp_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)

            if (e.Control && e.KeyCode == Keys.D)
            {

            }
        }
