        }
        public void Refresh_Final_Values()
        {
            GridSummaryItem summery_item = gridView7.Columns["Total"].SummaryItem;
            summery_item.SummaryType = DevExpress.Data.SummaryItemType.Sum;
            Double sub_totals = Convert.ToDouble(summery_item.SummaryValue);
            total_amount_inp.Text = string.Format("{0:#,##0.00}", Double.Parse(sub_totals.ToString()));
        }
        private void gridView7_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {
            if ((e.KeyCode == Keys.Delete) || (e.Control && e.KeyCode == Keys.X))
            {
                gridView7.DeleteRow(gridView7.FocusedRowHandle);
                if (gridView7.DataRowCount > 0)
                {
                    Refresh_Final_Values();
                }
                else
                {
                    Reset_Fields();
                }
            }
            else if ((e.Control && e.KeyCode == Keys.E) || (e.KeyCode == Keys.F2) && items_list_container.ContainsFocus == true)
            {
                if (gridView7.DataRowCount > 0)
                {
                    Int32 sr_no = Convert.ToInt32(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Sr.No"));
                    item_cmb.EditValue = sr_no;
                    sale_rate_inp.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Sale Rate"));
                    model_cmb.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Model"));
                    item_qty_inp.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Quantity"));
                    disc_percent_inp.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Disc(%)"));
                    disc_rs_inp.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Disc(R.S)"));
                    item_total.Text = Convert.ToString(gridView7.GetFocusedRowCellValue("Total"));
                    GridView view = item_cmb.Properties.View;
                    string fieldName = "Code";
                    int item_code = Convert.ToInt32(view.GetRowCellValue(item_cmb.Properties.GetIndexByKeyValue(item_cmb.EditValue), fieldName));
                    Items.Item item = items_controller.Get_Item(item_code);
                    model_cmb.EditValue = item.Model_Id;
                    int row_ind = gridView7.GetFocusedDataSourceRowIndex();
                    gridView7.DeleteRow(row_ind);
                    if (gridView7.DataRowCount > 0)
                    {
                        Refresh_Final_Values();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Set_Final_Values();
                    }

                    item_qty_inp.Focus();
                }
            }
            else
            {
                //Controls_List(sender, e);
            }
        }

        private void New_Aggrment_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            panelControl5.Size = new Size(panelControl5.Width, panelControl5.Height - panelControl6.Height);
            if (Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway == "API")
            {
                sms_gateway_cmb.Text = Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway;
            }
            else if (Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway == "Android Wifi")
            {
                sms_gateway_cmb.Text = Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway;
            }
            else if (Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway == "None")
            {
                sms_gateway_cmb.Text = Settings.Default.default_sms_gateway;
                sms_gateway_cmb.Enabled = false;
                send_msg_chkbx.Enabled = false;

            }
            messages_type_cmb.Text = Settings.Default.default_sms_language;
            if (Settings.Default.default_sms_language != "English")
            {
                foreach (InputLanguage lng in InputLanguage.InstalledInputLanguages)
                {
                    if (lng.Culture.DisplayName == "Urdu (Islamic Republic of Pakistan)")
                    {
                        InputLanguage.CurrentInputLanguage = lng;
                    }
                }
                message_inp.RightToLeft = RightToLeft.Yes;
                message_inp.Text = Settings.Default.new_agreement_urd_template;
            }
            else
            {
                foreach (InputLanguage lng in InputLanguage.InstalledInputLanguages)
                {
                    if (lng.Culture.DisplayName == "English (United States)")
                    {
                        InputLanguage.CurrentInputLanguage = lng;
                    }
                }
                message_inp.RightToLeft = RightToLeft.No;
                message_inp.Text = Settings.Default.new_agreement_eng_template;
            }
        }

        private void first_emi_inp_KeyPress(object sender, KeyPressEventArgs e)
        {
            Char ch = e.KeyChar;

            if (!(Char.IsDigit(ch) || ch == 13 || ch == 8 || ch == 46))

        public void Controls_List(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
        {

            if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Escape && (customer_cmb.Text != "" || store_cmb.Text != ""))
            {
                if (XtraMessageBox.Show("Are you sure, you want to cancel !", "Warning Alert", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo) != DialogResult.No)
                {
                    this.Reset_Fields();
                }
            }
            else if (e.KeyCode == Keys.F6 || (e.Control && e.Shift && e.KeyCode == Keys.C))
            {
                customer_cmb.Focus();
                int temp_store_id = -1;
                if (store_cmb.Text != "")
                {
                    temp_store_id = Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue);
                }
                Sales.New_Customer new_customer = new Sales.New_Customer(temp_store_id);
                new_customer.new_aggreement = this;
                new_customer.Do_Selection = true;

                UC_Opener_Form form = new UC_Opener_Form("New Customer", new_customer);
                new_customer.us_opener = form;
                form.Show(this);
            }
            else if (e.KeyCode == Keys.F7 || (e.Control && e.Shift && e.KeyCode == Keys.G))
            {
                guardian_cmb.Focus();
                int temp_store_id = -1;
                if (store_cmb.Text != "")
                {
                    temp_store_id = Convert.ToInt32(store_cmb.EditValue);
                }
                New_Guardian new_guardian = new New_Guardian(temp_store_id);
                new_guardian.Do_Selection = true;
                new_guardian.new_aggreement = this;
                UC_Opener_Form form = new UC_Opener_Form("New Guarantor", new_guardian);
                new_guardian.us_opener = form;
                form.Show();
            }
            else if (e.Control && e.KeyCode == Keys.S)
            {
                this.Save();
            }
        }
    }
}

-------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Data.SqlClient;
using DevExpress.XtraEditors;

namespace Jamera.Accounts
{
    class Accounts_Controller
    {
        SqlConnection conn = new SqlConnection();
        public Accounts_Controller()
        {
            DatabaseHandler db_handler = new DatabaseHandler();
            this.conn = db_handler.GetDatabseConnection();
        }

        public void Update_Account_Balance(int ac_id, double balance)
        {
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"UPDATE accounts SET balance='" + balance + "' WHERE id='" + ac_id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            conn.Close();
        }

        public void Update_Opening_Debit(int ac_id, double balance)
        {
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"UPDATE journal_transactions SET opening_debit="+balance+" WHERE account_id='"+ac_id+"' and id=(SELECT TOP 1 MIN(id) FROM journal_transactions WHERE account_id='"+ac_id+"')";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            conn.Close();
        }
        public void Update_Opening_Credit(int ac_id, double balance)
        {
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"UPDATE journal_transactions SET opening_credit=" + balance + " WHERE account_id='" + ac_id + "' and id=(SELECT TOP 1 MIN(id) FROM journal_transactions WHERE account_id='" + ac_id + "')";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            conn.Close();
        }
        public int Exists_Transaction(int ac_id)
        {
            int result = -1;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT COUNT(account_id) FROM journal_transactions WHERE account_id='" + ac_id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value)
            {
                result = Convert.ToInt32(obj);
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public int Exists_Ref_No(String ref_no)
        {
            int result = -1;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT TOP 1 debit_account_id FROM vouchers WHERE ref_no='"+ref_no+ "' and debit_description='Opening Sales'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value)
            {
                result = Convert.ToInt32(obj);
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public Double Get_Opening_Balance_Ref_No(String ref_no)
        {
            Double result = -1;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT TOP 1 amount FROM vouchers WHERE ref_no='" + ref_no + "' and debit_description='Opening Sales'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value)
            {
                result = Convert.ToDouble(obj);
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public Double Get_Current_Balance_Opening_Entries_Ref_No(int customer_id,String ref_no)
        {
            Double result =0;
            Double total_credit = 0;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT SUM(amount) FROM vouchers WHERE ref_no='"+ref_no+ "' and voucher_type='Reciept Voucher'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value)
            {
                total_credit = Convert.ToDouble(obj);
            }
            conn.Close();
            Double opening_balance= Get_Opening_Balance_Ref_No(ref_no);
            result = (opening_balance - total_credit);
            return result;
        }
        public String Get_Account_Type(int ac_id)
        {
            conn.Open();

            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            String res = null;
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value || obj != null)
            {
                res = Convert.ToString(obj);
            }
            conn.Close();
            return res;
        }
        public bool Is_Account_Editable(int ac_id)
        {
            bool result= true;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT created_type FROM accounts WHERE id='" + ac_id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value || obj != null)
            {
                if(Convert.ToString(obj) == "System")
                {
                    result = false;
                }
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }

        public int Store(String NAME,String EMAIL,int AC_GROUP_ID,String AC_GROUP,String AC_TYPE,String PHONE,String ADDRESS, String DESCRIPTION,Double BALANCE)
        {
            int result = -1;
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("INSERT INTO accounts(name,email,phone,address,account_group_id,account_group,remarks,account_type,balance,created_type) values(@name,@email,@phone,@address,@account_group_id,@account_group,@remarks,@account_type,@balance,@created_type)  SELECT CAST(scope_identity() AS int)", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", NAME);
                if(EMAIL == null)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", EMAIL);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_group_id", AC_GROUP_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_group", AC_GROUP);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@remarks", DESCRIPTION);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@phone", PHONE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@address", ADDRESS);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_type", AC_TYPE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@balance", BALANCE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created_type", "Custom");
                object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
                if (obj != DBNull.Value || obj != null)
                {
                    result = Convert.ToInt32(obj);
                }
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }

        public int Store(String NAME, String EMAIL, int AC_GROUP_ID, String AC_GROUP, String AC_TYPE, String PHONE, String ADDRESS, String DESCRIPTION, Double BALANCE, int STORE_ID, String FATHER_NAME,int AGE,String COST,String PROFESSION,String ID_CARD_NO,String BUSINESS_ADDRESS,String HOME_ADDRESS,String HOME_PHONE)
        {
            int result = -1;
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("INSERT INTO accounts(name,email,phone,address,account_group_id,account_group,remarks,account_type,store_id,father_name,age,cost,profession,id_card_no,business_address,home_address,home_phone,balance,created_type) values(@name,@email,@phone,@address,@account_group_id,@account_group,@remarks,@account_type,@store_id,@father_name,@age,@cost,@profession,@id_card_no,@business_address,@home_address,@home_phone,@balance,@created_type)  SELECT CAST(scope_identity() AS int)", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", NAME);
                if (EMAIL == null)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", EMAIL);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_group_id", AC_GROUP_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_group", AC_GROUP);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@remarks", DESCRIPTION);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@phone", PHONE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@address", ADDRESS);

                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@balance", BALANCE);

                {

        public int Get_Serial_Number()
        {
            int result = -1;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT MAX(id) from accounts;";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value)
            {
                result = Convert.ToInt32(obj) + 1;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public bool Update(int ID, String NAME, String EMAIL, int AC_GROUP_ID, String AC_GROUP, String AC_TYPE, String PHONE, String ADDRESS, String DESCRIPTION,int STORE_ID, String FATHER_NAME, int AGE, String COST, String PROFESSION, String ID_CARD_NO, String BUSINESS_ADDRESS, String HOME_ADDRESS, String HOME_PHONE)
        {
            bool result = false;
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("UPDATE accounts SET name=@name,email=@email,phone=@phone,address=@address,account_group_id=@account_group_id,account_group=@account_group,remarks=@remarks,account_type=@account_type,store_id=@store_id,father_name=@father_name,age=@age,cost=@cost,profession=@profession,id_card_no=@id_card_no,business_address=@business_address,home_address=@home_address,home_phone=@home_phone WHERE id=@id", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id",ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", NAME);
                if (EMAIL == null)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", EMAIL);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_group_id", AC_GROUP_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_group", AC_GROUP);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@remarks", DESCRIPTION);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@phone", PHONE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@address", ADDRESS);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_type", AC_TYPE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created_type", "Custom");
                if(STORE_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@store_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@store_id", STORE_ID);
                }
                
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@father_name", FATHER_NAME);
                if (AGE != -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@age", AGE);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@age", DBNull.Value);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cost", COST);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@profession", PROFESSION);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id_card_no", ID_CARD_NO);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@business_address", BUSINESS_ADDRESS);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@home_address", HOME_ADDRESS);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@home_phone", HOME_PHONE);
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
                result = true;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public bool Update(int ID, String NAME, String EMAIL, int AC_GROUP_ID, String AC_GROUP, String AC_TYPE, String PHONE, String ADDRESS, String DESCRIPTION)
        {
            bool result = false;
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("UPDATE accounts SET name=@name,email=@email,phone=@phone,address=@address,account_group_id=@account_group_id,account_group=@account_group,remarks=@remarks,account_type=@account_type WHERE id=@id", conn))
            {

                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", NAME);
                if (EMAIL == null)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@email", EMAIL);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_group_id", AC_GROUP_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_group", AC_GROUP);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@remarks", DESCRIPTION);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@phone", PHONE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@address", ADDRESS);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_type", AC_TYPE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@created_type", "Custom");
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();

            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public bool Delete(int ID)
        {
            conn.Open();
            bool result = false;
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("DELETE FROM accounts WHERE id=@id", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", ID);
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
                result = true;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }

        public bool Exists(String name)
        {
            bool result = false;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE name='" + name + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object query_results = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (query_results == DBNull.Value || query_results == null)
            {
                result = false;
            }
            else
            {
                result = true;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public bool Exists(int ac_id,String name)
        {
            bool result = false;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE name='" + name + "' and id !='"+ac_id+"'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object query_results = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (query_results != DBNull.Value && query_results != null)
            {
                 if(Convert.ToInt32(query_results) == ac_id)
                {
                    result = true;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                result = false;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }

        public String Get_Account_Nature(int ac_id)
        {
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT ac_gps.nature from accounts right join account_groups as ac_gps on accounts.account_group_id=ac_gps.id where accounts.id='" + ac_id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            String res = "";
            SqlDataReader reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
            while (reader.Read())
            {
                res = reader["nature"].ToString();
            }
            reader.Close();
            conn.Close();
            return res;
        }
        public Account Get_Account(int ac_id)
        {
            Account account = new Account();
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT TOP 1 accounts.id as 'ID',accounts.name as 'Name',accounts.phone as 'Phone',accounts.address as 'Address',ac_gps.id as 'Group Id',ac_gps.name as 'Group Name',ac_gps.nature as 'Nature',ac_gps.account_type as 'Account Type',accounts.balance as 'Balance',accounts.remarks as 'Remarks' from accounts left join account_groups as ac_gps on accounts.account_group_id=ac_gps.id where accounts.id='" + ac_id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            SqlDataReader reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
            while (reader.Read())
            {
                account.Account_Id = ac_id;
                account.Account_Name = reader["Name"].ToString();
                account.Account_Group_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["Group Id"]);
                account.Account_Group_Name = reader["Group Name"].ToString();
                account.Phone = reader["Phone"].ToString();
                account.Address = reader["Address"].ToString();
                account.Account_Nature = reader["Nature"].ToString();
                account.Account_Type = reader["Account Type"].ToString();
                account.Balance = Convert.ToDouble(reader["Balance"]);
                account.Account_Remarks = reader["Remarks"].ToString();
            }
            reader.Close();
            conn.Close();
            return account;
        }

        public Account Get_Customer(int ac_id)
        {
            Account account = new Account();
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT TOP 1 accounts.id as 'ID',accounts.store_id as 'Store Id',accounts.name as 'Name',accounts.father_name as 'Father Name',accounts.age as 'Age',accounts.cost as 'Cast',accounts.profession as 'Profession',accounts.id_card_no as 'Id Card',accounts.business_address as 'Business Address',accounts.home_address as 'Home Address',accounts.phone as 'Office Phone',accounts.home_phone as 'Home Phone',accounts.address as 'Permanent Address',accounts.balance as 'Balance',accounts.remarks as 'Remarks' from accounts left join account_groups as ac_gps on accounts.account_group_id=ac_gps.id where accounts.id='" + ac_id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            SqlDataReader reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
            while (reader.Read())
            {
                account.Account_Id = ac_id;
                if(reader["Store Id"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    account.Store_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["Store Id"]);
                }
                else
                {
                    account.Store_Id = -1;
                }
                if(reader["Age"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    account.Age = Convert.ToInt32(reader["Age"]);
                }
                else
                {
                    account.Age = -1;
                }                
                account.Account_Name = reader["Name"].ToString();
                account.Father_Name = reader["Father Name"].ToString();
                account.Cast = reader["Cast"].ToString();
                account.Profession = reader["Profession"].ToString();
                account.Id_Card_No = reader["Id Card"].ToString();
                account.Office_Phone = reader["Office Phone"].ToString();
                account.Home_Phone = reader["Home Phone"].ToString();
                account.Home_Address = reader["Home Address"].ToString();
                account.Office_Address = reader["Business Address"].ToString();
                account.Permanent_Address = reader["Permanent Address"].ToString();
                account.Balance = Convert.ToDouble(reader["Balance"]);
                account.Account_Remarks = reader["Remarks"].ToString();
            }
            reader.Close();
            conn.Close();
            return account;
        }

        public void Insert_Account_Transaction(int AC_ID, String AC_NAME,String AC_TYPE,String AC_NATURE, String SOURCE, String DESCRIPTION, double OPENING_DEBIT, double OPENING_CREDIT, double DEBIT, double CREDIT, double BALANCE, DateTime DATE, String INVOICE_NO = null, String ITEM_NAME = null, double ITEM_QUANTITY = 0,int CASH_VOUCHER_ID=-1,int STORE_ID=-1)
        {
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("Insert into journal_transactions(account_type,description,source,system_date,entry_date,account_id,account_name,opening_debit,opening_credit,debit,credit,closing_balance,invoice_no,item_name,item_quantity,account_nature,cash_voucher_id,store_id) values(@account_type,@description,@source,@system_date,@entry_date,@account_id,@account_name,@opening_debit,@opening_credit,@debit,@credit,@closing_balance,@invoice_no,@item_name,@item_quantity,@account_nature,@cash_voucher_id,@store_id)", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_type", AC_TYPE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_nature", AC_NATURE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@description", DESCRIPTION);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@source", SOURCE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@system_date", DateTime.Now);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@entry_date", DATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_id", AC_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@account_name", AC_NAME);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@opening_debit", OPENING_DEBIT);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@opening_credit", OPENING_CREDIT);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@debit", DEBIT);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@credit", CREDIT);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@closing_balance", BALANCE);
                if(CASH_VOUCHER_ID != -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cash_voucher_id", CASH_VOUCHER_ID);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cash_voucher_id", DBNull.Value);
                }

                if(STORE_ID != -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@store_id", STORE_ID);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@store_id",DBNull.Value);
                }

                if (INVOICE_NO != null)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@invoice_no", INVOICE_NO);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@invoice_no", DBNull.Value);
                }
                if (ITEM_NAME != null)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@item_name", ITEM_NAME);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@item_name", DBNull.Value);
                }

                if (ITEM_QUANTITY != 0)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@item_quantity", ITEM_QUANTITY);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@item_quantity", DBNull.Value);
                }

                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
                conn.Close();
                Update_Account_Balance(AC_ID, BALANCE);
            }
        }
        public bool Customer_Exists(String name,int store_id)
        {
            bool result = false;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE name='" + name + "' and store_id ='" + store_id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object query_results = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (query_results == DBNull.Value || query_results == null)
            {
                result = false;
            }
            else
            {
                result = true;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public bool Customer_Exists(int customer_id,String name, int store_id)
        {
            bool result = false;
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE name='" + name + "' and store_id ='" + store_id + "' and id!='"+customer_id+"'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object query_results = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (query_results == DBNull.Value || query_results == null)
            {
                result = false;
            }
            else
            {
                if(Convert.ToInt32(query_results) == customer_id)
                {
                    result = true;
                }
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
    }
}

------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Data.SqlClient;
using DevExpress.XtraEditors;

namespace Jamera.Items
{
    class Items_Controller
    {
        SqlConnection conn = new SqlConnection();
        public Items_Controller()
        {
            DatabaseHandler db_handler = new DatabaseHandler();
            this.conn = db_handler.GetDatabseConnection();
        }

        public int Store(String NAME, String UNICODE, Double PURCHASE_RATE , Double SALE_RATE , Double UNIT_COST , Double PACKING, int BARCODE_ID=-1, Byte PICTURE=Byte.MinValue,int UNIT_ID=-1,int GROUP_ID=-1,int CATEGORY_ID=-1,int MANUFACTURER_ID=-1,int DEFAULT_VENDOR_ID=-1,Double GST_PERCENT=0,Double SALE_DISCOUNT=0,Double REORDER_LEVEL=0,Double QUANTITY_DEMAND=0,bool IS_EXPIREABLE=false,bool IS_IMPORTED=false,bool IS_ACTIVE=true,bool IS_COUNTER_SALE=false,int RACK_ID=-1,Double LOCATION=-1,int Model_Id=-1, int NO_OF_INSTALLMENTS = 0, double ADVANCE_RATE = 0, double EMI_RATE = 0, double CASH_RATE = 0)
        {
            int result = -1;
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("INSERT INTO items(name,unicode_name,barcode_id,picture,unit_id,packing,group_id,category_id,manufacturer_id,vendor_id,purchase_rate,advance_rate,emi_price, no_of_installments, cash_rate,sale_rate,unit_cost,gst,sale_discount,reorder_level,demand,is_expireable,is_imported,is_active,is_counter_sale,rack_id,location,model_id) values(@name,@unicode_name,@barcode_id,@picture,@unit_id,@packing,@group_id,@category_id,@manufacturer_id,@vendor_id,@purchase_rate,@advance_rate,@emi_price, @no_of_installments, @cash_rate,@sale_rate,@unit_cost,@gst,@sale_discount,@reorder_level,@demand,@is_expireable,@is_imported,@is_active,@is_counter_sale,@rack_id,@location,@model_id) SELECT CAST(scope_identity() AS int)", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", NAME);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unicode_name", UNICODE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase_rate", PURCHASE_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@advance_rate", ADVANCE_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@emi_price", EMI_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@no_of_installments", NO_OF_INSTALLMENTS);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@cash_rate", CASH_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sale_rate", SALE_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit_cost", UNIT_COST);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@packing", PACKING);
                if (BARCODE_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@barcode_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@barcode_id", BARCODE_ID);
                }
                if (Model_Id == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@model_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@model_id", Model_Id);
                }
                if (PICTURE == Byte.MinValue)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@picture", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@picture", PICTURE);
                }
                if(UNIT_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit_id", UNIT_ID);
                }
                if (GROUP_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@group_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@group_id", GROUP_ID);
                }
                if (CATEGORY_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@category_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@category_id", CATEGORY_ID);
                }
                if (MANUFACTURER_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@manufacturer_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@manufacturer_id", MANUFACTURER_ID);
                }

                if (DEFAULT_VENDOR_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@vendor_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@vendor_id", DEFAULT_VENDOR_ID);
                }
                if (RACK_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@rack_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@rack_id", RACK_ID);
                }
                if (LOCATION == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@location", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@location", LOCATION);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@gst", GST_PERCENT);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sale_discount", SALE_DISCOUNT);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@reorder_level", REORDER_LEVEL);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@demand", QUANTITY_DEMAND);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@is_expireable", IS_EXPIREABLE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@is_imported", IS_IMPORTED);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@is_active", IS_ACTIVE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@is_counter_sale",IS_COUNTER_SALE);

                object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
                if (obj != DBNull.Value || obj != null)
                {
                    result = Convert.ToInt32(obj);
                }
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }

        public int Get_Serial_Number()
        {
            int result = -1;
            String query = @"SELECT MAX(id) from items;";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value)
            {
                result = Convert.ToInt32(obj) + 1;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }
        public bool Update(int ID,String NAME, String UNICODE, Double PURCHASE_RATE, Double SALE_RATE, Double UNIT_COST, Double PACKING, int BARCODE_ID = -1, Byte PICTURE = Byte.MinValue, int UNIT_ID = -1, int GROUP_ID = -1, int CATEGORY_ID = -1, int MANUFACTURER_ID = -1, int DEFAULT_VENDOR_ID = -1, Double GST_PERCENT = 0, Double SALE_DISCOUNT = 0, Double REORDER_LEVEL = 0, Double QUANTITY_DEMAND = 0, bool IS_EXPIREABLE = false, bool IS_IMPORTED = false, bool IS_ACTIVE = true, bool IS_COUNTER_SALE = false, int RACK_ID = -1, Double LOCATION = -1,int Model_Id=-1, int NO_OF_INSTALLMENTS = 0, double ADVANCE_RATE = 0, double EMI_RATE = 0, double CASH_RATE = 0)
        {
            conn.Open();
            bool result = false;
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("UPDATE items SET name=@name,unicode_name=@unicode_name,barcode_id=@barcode_id,picture=@picture,unit_id=@unit_id,packing=@packing,group_id=@group_id,category_id=@category_id,manufacturer_id=@manufacturer_id,vendor_id=@vendor_id,purchase_rate=@purchase_rate,emi_price=@emi_price, advance_rate=@advance_rate, no_of_installments=@no_of_installments, cash_rate=@cash_rate,sale_rate=@sale_rate,unit_cost=@unit_cost,gst=@gst,sale_discount=@sale_discount,reorder_level=@reorder_level,demand=@demand,is_expireable=@is_expireable,is_imported=@is_imported,is_active=@is_active,is_counter_sale=@is_counter_sale,rack_id=@rack_id,location=@location,model_id=@model_id WHERE id=@id", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ID", ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", NAME);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unicode_name", UNICODE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase_rate", PURCHASE_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@advance_rate", EMI_RATE);

                if (BARCODE_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@barcode_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {

                if (Model_Id == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@model_id", DBNull.Value);

                {

                if (PICTURE == Byte.MinValue)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@picture", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@picture", PICTURE);
                }
                if (UNIT_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@unit_id", UNIT_ID);
                }
                if (GROUP_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@group_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@group_id", GROUP_ID);
                }
                if (CATEGORY_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@category_id", DBNull.Value);

                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@category_id", CATEGORY_ID);

                if (MANUFACTURER_ID == -1)

                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@manufacturer_id", MANUFACTURER_ID);
                }

                if (DEFAULT_VENDOR_ID == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@vendor_id", DBNull.Value);

                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@vendor_id", DEFAULT_VENDOR_ID);

        public bool Delete(int ID)
        {
            conn.Open();
            bool result = false;
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("DELETE FROM items WHERE id=@id", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@id", ID);
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
                result = true;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }

        public bool Exists(String name,int Model_Id=-1)
        {
            conn.Open();
            bool result = false;
            String query = null;
            if (Model_Id == -1)
            {
                query = @"SELECT id FROM items WHERE name='" + name + "'  and model_id is NULL";
            }
            else
            {
                query = @"SELECT id FROM items WHERE name='" + name + "'  and model_id='"+Model_Id+"'";
            }
            
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object query_results = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (query_results == DBNull.Value || query_results == null)
            {
                result = false;
            }
            else
            {
                result = true;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }

        public bool Exists(int id, String name,int Model_Id)
        {
            conn.Open();
            bool result = false;
            String query = null;
            if (Model_Id == -1)
            {
                query = @"SELECT id FROM items WHERE name='" + name + "' and id !='" + id + "'  and model_id is NULL";
            }
            else
            {
                query = @"SELECT id FROM items WHERE name='" + name + "' and id !='" + id + "'  and model_id ='"+Model_Id+"'";
            }
            
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object query_results = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            if (query_results == DBNull.Value || query_results == null)
            {
                result = false;
            }
            else
            {
                result = true;
            }
            conn.Close();
            return result;
        }

        public Item Get_Item(int item_Id)
        {
            conn.Open();
            Item item = new Item();
            String query = @"SELECT y.purchase_id,items.id as item_id,items.name as item_name,items.unicode_name as unicode_name,i_u.id as unit_id,i_u.name as unit_name,items.model_id,models.name as model_name,items.packing as packing,items.purchase_rate as purchase_rate,items.sale_rate as sale_rate,items.unit_cost as unit_cost,items.sale_discount as sale_discount,items.is_active as is_active,items.reorder_level as reorder_level ,i_m.id as manufacturer_id,i_m.name as manufacturer_name,i_g.id as group_id,i_g.name as group_name,i_g_s.id as category_id,i_g_s.name as category_name,COALESCE(SUM(y.items_stock_in - y.items_stock_out),0) as item_stock from items left join item_manufacturers  i_m on i_m.id=items.manufacturer_id left join item_groups  i_g on i_g.id=items.group_id left join item_categories as i_g_s on i_g_s.id=items.category_id left join item_units as i_u on i_u.id=items.unit_id left join purchase_items as y on items.id=y.item_id left join item_models as models on items.model_id=models.id  WHERE items.id='" + item_Id+"' group by items.id ,items.name,items.unicode_name,i_u.id,i_u.name,items.packing,items.purchase_rate,items.sale_rate ,items.unit_cost,items.sale_discount,items.is_active,items.reorder_level ,i_m.id,i_m.name,i_g.id,i_g.name ,i_g_s.id,i_g_s.name,y.purchase_id,models.name,items.model_id ";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query,conn);
            SqlDataReader reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
            while (reader.Read())
            {
                item.Id = item_Id;
                item.Packing = Convert.ToDouble(reader["packing"]);
                item.Name = reader["item_name"].ToString();
                item.Unicode = reader["unicode_name"].ToString();
                if(reader["purchase_id"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    item.Purchase_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["purchase_id"]);
                }
                item.Purchase_Rate = Convert.ToDouble(reader["purchase_rate"]);
                item.Sale_Rate = Convert.ToDouble(reader["sale_rate"]);
                item.Unit_Cost = Convert.ToDouble(reader["unit_cost"]);
                item.Sale_Discount = Convert.ToDouble(reader["sale_discount"]);
                item.Reorder_Level = Convert.ToDouble(reader["reorder_level"]);
                if(reader["is_active"].ToString() == "1")
                {
                    item.Is_Active = true;
                }
                else
                {
                    item.Is_Active = false;
                }
                if (reader["unit_id"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    item.Unit_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["unit_id"]);
                    item.Unit_Name = reader["unit_name"].ToString();
                }
                else
                {
                    item.Unit_Name = null;
                }
                if (reader["model_id"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    item.Model_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["model_id"]);
                    item.Model_Name = reader["model_name"].ToString();
                }
                else
                {
                    item.Model_Name = null;
                }
                if (reader["group_id"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    item.Group_id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["group_id"]);
                    item.Group_Name = reader["group_name"].ToString();
                }
                else
                {
                    item.Group_Name = null;
                }
                if (reader["manufacturer_id"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    item.Manufacturer_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["manufacturer_id"]);
                    item.Manufacturer_Name = reader["manufacturer_name"].ToString();
                }
                else
                {
                    item.Manufacturer_Name = null;
                }
                if (reader["category_id"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    item.Category_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["category_id"]);
                    item.Category_Name = reader["category_name"].ToString();
                }
                else
                {
                    item.Category_Name = null;
                }
            }
            reader.Close();
            conn.Close();
            return item;
        }
    }

using DevExpress.DashboardCommon.Native;
using DevExpress.XtraEditors;
using Jamera.Reports.Stocks;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.SqlClient;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Jamera.Items
{
    class Stocks_Controller
    {
        SqlConnection conn = new SqlConnection();
        Accounts.Accounts_Controller accounts_controller = new Accounts.Accounts_Controller();
        public Stocks_Controller()
        {
            DatabaseHandler db_handler = new DatabaseHandler();
            this.conn = db_handler.GetDatabseConnection();
        }

        public Double Get_Items_Stock_At_Store(int ITEM_ID, int STORE_ID)
        {
            conn.Open();
            Double result = 0;
            // Updated calculation: add sales_return, subtract others
            String query = @"SELECT SUM((sti.stock_in + sti.sales_return) - (sti.sold_out + sti.returned + sti.transfered)) 
                     FROM stock_transaction_items as sti 
                     WHERE sti.item_id='" + ITEM_ID + "' and sti.store_id='" + STORE_ID + "';";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            conn.Close();
            if (obj != DBNull.Value && obj != null) result = Convert.ToDouble(obj);
            return result;
        }

        public DataTable Get_Available_Stock_At_Store(int STORE_ID)
        {
            DataTable available_Stock_Table = new DataTable();
            conn.Open();

            // Updated calculation: add sales_return, subtract others
            String query = @"SELECT rank() over(order by sti.item_id,items.name,sti.sale_rate) as 'Sr.No',sti.item_id as 'Code',items.name as 'Item',models.name as 'Model',sti.sale_rate as 'Sale Rate',SUM((sti.stock_in + sti.sales_return) - (sti.sold_out + sti.returned + sti.transfered)) as 'Stock',i_u.name as 'U.O.M' FROM stock_transaction_items as sti left join items on sti.item_id=items.id left join item_units as i_u on items.unit_id=i_u.id left join item_models as models on items.model_id=models.id WHERE ((sti.stock_in - (sti.sold_out + sti.returned + sti.transfered) ) > 0) and sti.store_id='" + STORE_ID + "' GROUP BY sti.item_id,items.name,sti.sale_rate,i_u.name,models.name";
            SqlDataAdapter adp = new SqlDataAdapter(query, conn);
            adp.Fill(available_Stock_Table);
            conn.Close();
            return available_Stock_Table;
        }

        public Stock_Transfer_Instance Get_Stock_Transfer_Instance(int Stock_Transfer_Instance_Id)
        {
            Stock_Transfer_Instance stock_transfer_instance = new Stock_Transfer_Instance();
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT st.id,st.from_store,from_stores.name as from_store_name,from_stores.area_code as from_area_code,st.to_store,to_stores.name as to_store_name,to_stores.area_code as to_area_code,st.date as date,st.name as name,st.remarks FROM stock_transactions as st left join stores as from_stores on st.from_store=from_stores.id left join stores as to_stores on st.to_store=to_stores.id WHERE st.id='"+Stock_Transfer_Instance_Id+"';";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query,conn);
            SqlDataReader reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
            while (reader.Read())
            {
                stock_transfer_instance.Stock_Transfer_Id=Convert.ToInt32(reader["id"]);
                if(reader["from_store_name"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transfer_instance.From_Store = reader["from_store_name"].ToString();
                }
                if (reader["from_area_code"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transfer_instance.From_Area_Code = reader["from_area_code"].ToString();
                }
                if (reader["from_store"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transfer_instance.From_Store_Id =Convert.ToInt32(reader["from_store"]);
                }

                if (reader["to_store_name"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transfer_instance.To_Store = reader["to_store_name"].ToString();
                }
                if (reader["to_area_code"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transfer_instance.To_Area_Code = reader["to_area_code"].ToString();
                }
                if (reader["to_store"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transfer_instance.To_Store_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["to_store"]);
                }
                if (reader["remarks"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transfer_instance.Remarks = Convert.ToString(reader["remarks"]);
                }
                if (reader["date"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transfer_instance.Remarks = Convert.ToString(reader["date"]);
                }
            }
            reader.Close();
            conn.Close();
            //stock_transfer_instance.Stock_Transfer_Items_Table = Get_Stock_Transaction_Items(Stock_Transfer_Instance_Id);
            return stock_transfer_instance;
        }
        public int Store_Stock_Transaction(DateTime DATE,int FROM_STORE,int TO_STORE,String NAME,String REMARKS,DataTable ITEMS_TABLE,int Paid_Ac_Id=-1,Double Fuel_Expenses_Amount=0)
        {
            conn.Open();
            int result = -1;
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("INSERT INTO stock_transactions(date,from_store,to_store,name,remarks,paid_by_ac_id,fuel_expenses_amount) values(@date,@from_store,@to_store,@name,@remarks,@paid_by_ac_id,@fuel_expenses_amount) SELECT CAST(scope_identity() AS int)", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", NAME);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@remarks", REMARKS);
                if (FROM_STORE != -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@from_store", FROM_STORE);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@from_store", DBNull.Value);
                }
                if(Paid_Ac_Id == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@paid_by_ac_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@paid_by_ac_id", Paid_Ac_Id);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("fuel_expenses_amount",Fuel_Expenses_Amount);
                if(Fuel_Expenses_Amount > 0)
                {
                    double payable_account_current_balance = 0;
                    double payable_account_curr_balance = 0;
                    if (accounts_controller.Get_Account_Type(Paid_Ac_Id) == "CR")
                    {
                        payable_account_curr_balance = accounts_controller.Get_Account_Balance(Paid_Ac_Id);
                        payable_account_current_balance = (payable_account_curr_balance + Fuel_Expenses_Amount);
                        accounts_controller.Insert_Account_Transaction(Paid_Ac_Id, accounts_controller.Get_Account_Name(Paid_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Type(Paid_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Nature(Paid_Ac_Id), "#Stock Transfer", "Fuel Expenses", 0, 0, 0, Fuel_Expenses_Amount, payable_account_current_balance, DateTime.Now);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        payable_account_curr_balance = accounts_controller.Get_Account_Balance(Paid_Ac_Id);
                        payable_account_current_balance = (payable_account_curr_balance - Fuel_Expenses_Amount);
                        accounts_controller.Insert_Account_Transaction(Paid_Ac_Id, accounts_controller.Get_Account_Name(Paid_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Type(Paid_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Nature(Paid_Ac_Id), "#Stock Transfer", "Fuel Expenses", 0, 0, 0, Fuel_Expenses_Amount, payable_account_current_balance, DateTime.Now);
                    }
                    Extra_Resources.Extra_Veriables extra_veriables = new Extra_Resources.Extra_Veriables();
                    int Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id = extra_veriables.Fuel_Expnses_Ac_Id;
                    // here will be change
                    double fuel_expense_account_current_balance = 0;
                    double fuel_expense_account_curr_balance = 0;
                    fuel_expense_account_curr_balance = accounts_controller.Get_Account_Balance(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id);
                    fuel_expense_account_current_balance = (fuel_expense_account_curr_balance + Fuel_Expenses_Amount);
                    accounts_controller.Insert_Account_Transaction(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id, accounts_controller.Get_Account_Name(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Type(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Nature(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id), "#Purchase Bill", "Fuel Expenses", 0, 0, Fuel_Expenses_Amount, 0, fuel_expense_account_current_balance, DateTime.Now);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@to_store", TO_STORE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@date", DATE.ToString("dd-MM-yyyy"));
                object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
                conn.Close();
                if (obj != DBNull.Value || obj != null)
                {
                    result = Convert.ToInt32(obj);

                    if(FROM_STORE == -1)
                    {
                        //If FROM store is null 
                        for (int i = 0; i < ITEMS_TABLE.Rows.Count; i++)
                        {
                            int item_id = Convert.ToInt32(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i][1]);
                            Double item_qty = Convert.ToDouble(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i]["Quantity"]);
                            Update_Purchase_Stock(DATE,item_id, Convert.ToDouble(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i]["Sale Rate"]), TO_STORE,result,item_qty);
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        // if store to store
                        for (int i = 0; i < ITEMS_TABLE.Rows.Count; i++)
                        {
                            int item_id = Convert.ToInt32(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i][1]);
                            Double item_qty = Convert.ToDouble(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i]["Quantity"]);
                            Update_Store_Stock(DATE, item_id, Convert.ToDouble(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i]["Sale Rate"]), FROM_STORE,TO_STORE, result, item_qty);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return result;
        }
        
        public void Update_Purchase_Stock(DateTime DATE,int item_id,Double Sale_Rate,int store_id,int trasnsaction_id, Double items_qty)
        {
            Purchases.Purchases_Controller purchases_controller = new Purchases.Purchases_Controller();
            Purchases.Purchase_Item purchase_item = new Purchases.Purchase_Item();
            purchase_item = purchases_controller.Get_Current_Purchase_Item_By_Sale_Rate(item_id,Sale_Rate);
            Double available_stock = (purchase_item.Stock_In - (purchase_item.Stock_Out + purchase_item.Returned_Items));
            Double updated_qty = 0;
            if( available_stock >= items_qty)
            {
                updated_qty = (purchase_item.Stock_Out +  items_qty);
            }
            else
            {
                updated_qty = (purchase_item.Stock_Out + available_stock);
            }
            //Updating Purchases Stock Which required
            this.conn.Open();
            String query = @"UPDATE purchase_items SET items_stock_out='" + updated_qty + "' WHERE id='" + purchase_item.Purchase_Item_Id+ "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            this.conn.Close();
            //Store Transaction Item History
            if (available_stock >= items_qty)
            {
                Store_Stock_Transaction_Item(DATE, trasnsaction_id, store_id, purchase_item.Purchase_Id, purchase_item.Purchase_Item_Id, item_id, purchase_item.Purchase_Rate, purchase_item.Sale_Rate, items_qty, 0, 0, 0);
            }
            else
            {
                Store_Stock_Transaction_Item(DATE, trasnsaction_id, store_id, purchase_item.Purchase_Id, purchase_item.Purchase_Item_Id, item_id, purchase_item.Purchase_Rate, purchase_item.Sale_Rate, available_stock, 0, 0, 0);
            }
            items_qty = items_qty - available_stock;
            //if required qty is greater then available in record then check next
            if (items_qty > 0)
            {
                this.Update_Purchase_Stock(DATE,item_id,Sale_Rate,store_id,trasnsaction_id,items_qty);
            }
        }

        public void Update_Purchase_Returned_Stock(int purchase_item_id, Double items_qty)
        {
            Purchases.Purchases_Controller purchases_controller = new Purchases.Purchases_Controller();
            Purchases.Purchase_Item purchase_item = new Purchases.Purchase_Item();
            purchase_item = purchases_controller.Get_Purchase_Item_Through_Purchase_Item_Id(purchase_item_id);
            Double updated_qty = (purchase_item.Stock_Out - items_qty);
            //Updating Purchases Stock Which required
            this.conn.Open();
            String query = @"UPDATE purchase_items SET items_stock_out='" + updated_qty + "' WHERE id='" + purchase_item.Purchase_Item_Id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            this.conn.Close();
            //Store Transaction Item History
            
        }

        public Stock_Transaction Get_Stock_Transaction(int Stock_Transaction_Id)
        {
            Stock_Transaction stock_transaction = new Stock_Transaction();
            conn.Open();
            String query= @"select st.date as 'Date',st.id as 'Code',st.name as 'Name',st.remarks as 'Remarks',from_stores.id as 'From.Store.Id',from_stores.name as 'From.Store',from_stores.area_code as 'From.Area.Code',to_stores.id as 'To.Store.Id',to_stores.name as 'To.Store',to_stores.area_code as 'To.Area.Code' FROM stock_transactions as st left join stores as from_stores on  st.from_store=from_stores.id left join stores as to_stores on st.to_store=to_stores.id WHERE st.id='" + Stock_Transaction_Id+"';";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query,conn);
            SqlDataReader reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
            while (reader.Read())
            {
                stock_transaction.Date = reader["Date"].ToString();
                stock_transaction.Stock_Transaction_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["Code"]);
                if(reader["Name"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transaction.Name = reader["Name"].ToString();
                }
                if(reader["Remarks"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transaction.Remarks = reader["Remarks"].ToString();
                }
                if(reader["From.Store.Id"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transaction.From_Store_Id=Convert.ToInt32(reader["From.Store.Id"]);
                }
                if (reader["From.Store"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transaction.From_Store_Name = Convert.ToString(reader["From.Store"]);
                }
                if (reader["From.Area.Code"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transaction.From_Area_Code = Convert.ToString(reader["From.Area.Code"]);
                }
                stock_transaction.To_Store_Id = Convert.ToInt32(reader["To.Store.Id"]);
                stock_transaction.To_Store_Name = Convert.ToString(reader["To.Store"]);
                if(reader["To.Area.Code"] != DBNull.Value)
                {
                    stock_transaction.To_Area_Code = Convert.ToString(reader["To.Area.Code"]);
                }
            }
            reader.Close();
            conn.Close();
            stock_transaction.Stock_Transaction_Items_Table = Get_Stock_Transaction_Items(Stock_Transaction_Id);
            return stock_transaction;
        }
        public DataTable Get_Stock_Transaction_Items(int Stock_Transaction_Id)
        {
            DataTable Stock_Transaction_Items_Table = new DataTable();
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"SELECT items.id as 'Item Code',items.name as 'Item Name',models.name as 'Model',sti.sale_rate as 'Sale Rate',SUM(sti.stock_in) as 'Quantity' FROM stock_transaction_items as sti left join items on sti.item_id=items.id left join item_models as models on items.model_id=models.id WHERE sti.stock_transaction_id='" + Stock_Transaction_Id + "' group by items.id,items.name,sti.sale_rate,models.name;";
            SqlDataAdapter adp = new SqlDataAdapter(query,conn);
            adp.Fill(Stock_Transaction_Items_Table);
            conn.Close();
            return Stock_Transaction_Items_Table;
        }
        public void Update_Store_Stock(DateTime DATE, int item_id,Double Sale_Rate,int from_store, int store_id, int trasnsaction_id, Double items_qty)
        {
            Stores.Stores_Controller stores_controller = new Stores.Stores_Controller();
            Stores.Store_Item store_item = stores_controller.Get_Current_Stock_Item_By_Sale_Rate(item_id,from_store,Sale_Rate);
            Double available_qty = (store_item.Stock_in - (store_item.Sold_out + store_item.Transfered_Items + store_item.Returned_Items));
            Double updated_qty = 0;
            if(available_qty > items_qty)
            {
                updated_qty = (store_item.Transfered_Items + items_qty);
            }
            else
            {
                updated_qty = (store_item.Transfered_Items + available_qty);
            }
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"UPDATE stock_transaction_items SET transfered='" + updated_qty + "' WHERE id='" + store_item.Store_Item_Id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            conn.Close();
            if (available_qty > items_qty)
            {
                Store_Stock_Transaction_Item(DATE, trasnsaction_id, store_id, store_item.Purchase_Id, store_item.Purchase_Item_Id, item_id, store_item.Purchase_Rate, store_item.Sale_Rate, updated_qty, 0, 0, 0);
            }
            else
            {
                Store_Stock_Transaction_Item(DATE, trasnsaction_id, store_id, store_item.Purchase_Id, store_item.Purchase_Item_Id, item_id, store_item.Purchase_Rate, store_item.Sale_Rate, available_qty, 0, 0, 0);
            }
            items_qty = items_qty - available_qty;
            if (items_qty > 0)
            {
                this.Update_Store_Stock(DATE, item_id,Sale_Rate,from_store, store_id, trasnsaction_id, items_qty);
            }
            
        }
        public void Update_Store_Returned_Aggrement_Stock(int STOCK_TRANSACTION_ITEM_ID)
        {
            Stores.Stores_Controller stores_controller = new Stores.Stores_Controller();
            Stores.Store_Item store_item = stores_controller.Get_Stock_Item(STOCK_TRANSACTION_ITEM_ID);
            Double updated_qty = (store_item.Sold_out - 1);
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"UPDATE stock_transaction_items SET sold_out='" + updated_qty + "' WHERE id='" + STOCK_TRANSACTION_ITEM_ID + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            conn.Close();
        }

        public void Update_Store_Returned_Stock(DateTime DATE,int stock_return_id, int item_id,int store_id,Double Sale_Rate, Double items_qty)
        {
            Stores.Stores_Controller stores_controller = new Stores.Stores_Controller();
            Stores.Store_Item store_item = stores_controller.Get_Current_Stock_Item_By_Sale_Rate(item_id, store_id,Sale_Rate);
            Double available_qty = (store_item.Stock_in - (store_item.Sold_out + store_item.Transfered_Items + store_item.Returned_Items));
            Double updated_qty = 0;
            Double curr_stock_qty = 0;
            if (available_qty >= items_qty)
            {
                updated_qty = (store_item.Returned_Items + items_qty);
                curr_stock_qty = items_qty;
            }
            else
            {
                updated_qty = (store_item.Returned_Items + available_qty);
                curr_stock_qty = available_qty;
            }
            
            conn.Open();
            String query = @"UPDATE stock_transaction_items SET returned='" + updated_qty + "' WHERE id='" + store_item.Store_Item_Id + "'";
            SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand(query, conn);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            conn.Close();
            Store_Stock_Returned_Item(DATE,stock_return_id,store_item.Stock_Transaction_Id,store_id, store_item.Purchase_Id, store_item.Purchase_Item_Id, item_id, store_item.Purchase_Rate, store_item.Sale_Rate,curr_stock_qty);
            Update_Purchase_Returned_Stock(store_item.Purchase_Item_Id,curr_stock_qty);
            items_qty = items_qty - available_qty;
            if (items_qty > 0)
            {
                this.Update_Store_Returned_Stock(DATE,stock_return_id,item_id,store_id,Sale_Rate,items_qty);
            }
        }

        public void Store_Stock_Transaction_Item(DateTime DATE,int STOCK_TRANSACTION_ID,int STORE_ID, int PURCHASE_ID,int PURCHASE_ITEM_ID,int ITEM_ID,Double PURCHASE_RATE,Double SALE_RATE,Double STOCK_IN,Double SOLD_OUT,Double RETURNED,Double TRANSFERED)
        {
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("INSERT INTO stock_transaction_items(date,stock_transaction_id,purchase_id,store_id,item_id,purchase_rate,sale_rate,stock_in,sold_out,returned,transfered,purchase_item_id) values(@date,@stock_transaction_id,@purchase_id,@store_id,@item_id,@purchase_rate,@sale_rate,@stock_in,@sold_out,@returned,@transfered,@purchase_item_id) SELECT CAST(scope_identity() AS int)", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@date", DATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@stock_transaction_id", STOCK_TRANSACTION_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@store_id", STORE_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase_id", PURCHASE_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@item_id", ITEM_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase_rate", PURCHASE_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sale_rate",SALE_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@stock_in", STOCK_IN);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sold_out", SOLD_OUT);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@returned", RETURNED);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@transfered", TRANSFERED);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase_item_id",PURCHASE_ITEM_ID);
                object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            }
            conn.Close();
        }
        public void Store_Stock_Returned_Item(DateTime DATE,int STOCK_RETURN_ID, int STOCK_TRANSACTION_ID, int STORE_ID, int PURCHASE_ID, int PURCHASE_ITEM_ID, int ITEM_ID, Double PURCHASE_RATE, Double SALE_RATE, Double QUANTIY)
        {
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("INSERT INTO stock_return_items(date,stock_return_id,stock_transaction_id,purchase_id,store_id,item_id,purchase_rate,sale_rate,quantity,purchase_item_id) values(@date,@stock_return_id,@stock_transaction_id,@purchase_id,@store_id,@item_id,@purchase_rate,@sale_rate,@quantity,@purchase_item_id) SELECT CAST(scope_identity() AS int)", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@date", DATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@stock_return_id",STOCK_RETURN_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@stock_transaction_id", STOCK_TRANSACTION_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@store_id", STORE_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase_id", PURCHASE_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@item_id", ITEM_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase_rate", PURCHASE_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@sale_rate", SALE_RATE);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@quantity", QUANTIY);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@purchase_item_id", PURCHASE_ITEM_ID);
                object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
            }
            conn.Close();
        }
        public int Store_Stock_Return(DateTime DATE,int STORE_ID,String NAME,String DESCRIPTION,DataTable ITEMS_TABLE, int Paid_Ac_Id = -1, Double Fuel_Expenses_Amount = 0)
        {
            int result = -1;
            conn.Open();
            using (SqlCommand cmd = new SqlCommand("INSERT INTO stock_returns(date,store_id,name,description,paid_by_ac_id,fuel_expenses_amount) values(@date,@store_id,@name,@description,@paid_by_ac_id,@fuel_expenses_amount) SELECT CAST(scope_identity() AS int)", conn))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@name", NAME);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@store_id", STORE_ID);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@description",DESCRIPTION);
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@date", DATE.ToString("dd-MM-yyyy"));
                if (Paid_Ac_Id == -1)
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@paid_by_ac_id", DBNull.Value);
                }
                else
                {
                    cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@paid_by_ac_id", Paid_Ac_Id);
                }
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("fuel_expenses_amount", Fuel_Expenses_Amount);
                if (Fuel_Expenses_Amount > 0)
                {
                    double payable_account_current_balance = 0;
                    double payable_account_curr_balance = 0;
                    if (accounts_controller.Get_Account_Type(Paid_Ac_Id) == "CR")
                    {
                        payable_account_curr_balance = accounts_controller.Get_Account_Balance(Paid_Ac_Id);
                        payable_account_current_balance = (payable_account_curr_balance + Fuel_Expenses_Amount);
                        accounts_controller.Insert_Account_Transaction(Paid_Ac_Id, accounts_controller.Get_Account_Name(Paid_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Type(Paid_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Nature(Paid_Ac_Id), "#Stock Return", "Fuel Expenses", 0, 0, 0, Fuel_Expenses_Amount, payable_account_current_balance, DateTime.Now);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        payable_account_curr_balance = accounts_controller.Get_Account_Balance(Paid_Ac_Id);
                        payable_account_current_balance = (payable_account_curr_balance - Fuel_Expenses_Amount);
                        accounts_controller.Insert_Account_Transaction(Paid_Ac_Id, accounts_controller.Get_Account_Name(Paid_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Type(Paid_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Nature(Paid_Ac_Id), "#Stock Return", "Fuel Expenses", 0, 0, 0, Fuel_Expenses_Amount, payable_account_current_balance, DateTime.Now);
                    }

                    Extra_Resources.Extra_Veriables extra_veriables = new Extra_Resources.Extra_Veriables();
                    int Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id = extra_veriables.Fuel_Expnses_Ac_Id;
                    // here will be change
                    double fuel_expense_account_current_balance = 0;
                    double fuel_expense_account_curr_balance = 0;
                    fuel_expense_account_curr_balance = accounts_controller.Get_Account_Balance(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id);
                    fuel_expense_account_current_balance = (fuel_expense_account_curr_balance + Fuel_Expenses_Amount);
                    accounts_controller.Insert_Account_Transaction(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id, accounts_controller.Get_Account_Name(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Type(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id), accounts_controller.Get_Account_Nature(Fuel_Expense_Ac_Id), "#Stock Return", "Fuel Expenses", 0, 0, Fuel_Expenses_Amount, 0, fuel_expense_account_current_balance, DateTime.Now);
                }
                object obj = cmd.ExecuteScalar();
                conn.Close();
                if (obj != DBNull.Value || obj != null)
                {
                    result = Convert.ToInt32(obj);
                    //If store returned items
                    for (int i = 0; i < ITEMS_TABLE.Rows.Count; i++)
                    {
                        Update_Store_Returned_Stock(DATE,result, Convert.ToInt32(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i][1]), STORE_ID, Convert.ToDouble(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i]["Sale Rate"]), Convert.ToDouble(ITEMS_TABLE.Rows[i]["Quantity"]));
                    }
                }
            }

            return result;
        }
    }
}
